| `MEBP_v75x_SKETCH_ABSTRACT_INKS_RETRACE_QUICKPRINT_SWAPS.md` | v7.5.x **four operator-requested Print Builder → Sketch changes (one landing).** **(1) Optimizer path flexibility — retrace along an existing bead:** instead of a pen-up, when the next shape starts a *brief length* back along an already-printed bead (same ink, same Z) the needle stays DOWN and retraces there; opt-in `Sketch.overlap_travel_enabled`/`_max_mm`/`_pause_pump`/`_speed_factor` (conditional serialize → legacy byte-identical when off) + `SketchShape.retrace_from` (optimizer-stamped). New `_retrace_polyline`; compiler tracks `prev_print_path` + emits the retrace (first-pass gated; `move_to(speed, pump_creep)`); optimizer post-pass `_stamp_segment_retraces`. **CRITICAL round-trip:** a PAUSED retrace advances the pump by a negligible `pump_creep` (floored `>1e-9`/segment) so Quick Print's `_travel_mask` (`dp<=1e-9 & moving`→travel) never re-splits it into a lift+pressure-relief — "pause not stop." **(2) Abstract inks (pump-agnostic sketch):** NEW `SketchInk{id,name,color}` + `Sketch.inks`/`_next_ink_id` (+`ensure_default_ink`/`ink_by_id`/`ink_order_index`/`add_ink`/`rebuild_inks_from_shapes`); `SketchShape.pump_index`→**`ink_id`** (legacy migrate `pump_index`→`ink_id`=pump+1, verified on real `config/prints/*.json`). The 3 trajectory pump columns become a PREVIEW artifact keyed by `ink_order_index%3`; the single-needle weld break keys on the TRUE `ink_id` (`prev_print_ink`) so mod-3-colliding inks still break; unlimited inks OK (sequential swaps = one ink at a time). New Sketch-page **Inks** manager card (add/rename/recolor/delete + reassign-on-delete) + per-shape **Ink** picker replacing P1/P2/P3. **(3) Quick-Print runtime ink mapping + full sequential swaps:** a sketch using ≥2 abstract inks gets a mapping table (abstract ink → configured ink→pump, colour-defaulted, validated) + a one-worker engine: `run_prep` once → per ink-group (`aspirate_ink` mapped pump → discrete `PrintManager` job the worker blocks on) → `run_post_clean` (waste→wash→buffer) between → `run_print_cleanup` once → end at safe Z; reuses existing `PickPlaceExecutor` primitives (NOT the legacy `PrintPlanExecutor`/`InkSwapStrategy`). `_ink_groups` recompiles per-group sub-sketches (preserves welds/retrace within a group). `_build_settings(pump=)`; `_PrintBridge.multi_done`; abort/button/status wired. Single-ink & non-sketch prints keep the legacy path. Deferred (v1): multi-group syringe-budget pre-flight + per-group flow-ceiling precision. **(4) Right context panel rework:** one outer `QScrollArea` + PINNED Save/Send; the sequence card drops its fixed-height nested scroll; each print section is a COLLAPSIBLE content-sized block (chevron + clickable title→select shapes + per-shape op rows) that grows to fit; per-section collapse persisted. **Designed via 2 Explore + 2 Plan agents; AskUserQuestion chose unlimited-inks / full-swaps / retrace-along-bead.** **Needs GUI/HW verification on ME3B V1** (assign abstract inks + panel colours/collapse; Overlap-travel retrace holds pressure with no relief; multi-ink Quick Print maps + swaps + ends at safe Z). Tests: `test_v75x_sketch_abstract_inks.py` (17) + `test_v75x_sketch_panel_rework.py` (4) + `test_v75x_sketch_overlap_travel.py` (18, incl. `_travel_mask` round-trip guard) + `test_v75x_quick_print_multi_ink.py` (15); sketch (257) + quick-print (137) + adjacent (83) suites green. |
| `MEBP_v75x_ROSETTE_TAB_AUTO_REANCHOR_AND_MOSAIC_UI.md` | v7.5.x operator-feedback batch on plate calibration (follow-on to `SINGLE_WELL_MOSAIC_AND_GUIDED_MAPPING`). **BUG FIX — mapped centres offset by the registration shift:** `MosaicWellMappingDialog` back-projected clicks with the shift-INCLUDING `canvas_extent_um` (`MosaicBuilder._global_shift_um` — seeded from `MosaicAlignmentStore` and/or measured during stitching) while tile pixels sit at trusted raw stage positions; the detection path removes the shift (`_ploc_mosaic_world_shift`), the dialog didn't — so single-well/rosette sub-well AND full-plate "Map wells…" results were offset by it. Fix: `MosaicStore.save(shift_um=)` records the baked-in shift (+`get_shift_um`, legacy→(0,0); `update_extent` = metadata-only translate), all persist sites pass the live shift (captured BEFORE the builder is nulled in the finish path; `_ploc_shift_mosaic_by` preserves it on re-save), and every mapping path hands the dialog **extent − shift** (zero shift ⇒ byte-identical, regression-locked). Pre-fix saved mosaics have unknown baked shifts → re-scan. **Plate mapping = MAIN wells only:** `main_well_names(plate)` (sub-wells excluded; dropped rosette parents re-added at their sub-well centroid via `_nominal_position`; `corner_well_names(names=, positions=)` overrides); `_autofill`/refine-queue/auto-detect grid use `_map_names`; sub-wells calibrate ONLY via the Rosettes flow. **Four-button Mosaic group** (operator: "too many buttons"): `Calibrate mosaic scan` · `Mosaic scan` (full-plate finish now **auto-opens** the Map-wells dialog) · `Re-anchor mosaic` · `Auto re-anchor mosaic`, + Plate-view combo + "Well scans" toggle + **Advanced… ▾ menu** (Settings/Map wells/Quick re-register/Scan well/Re-register from scanned (N)/Re-derive/Manual-align-toggle) — legacy button ATTRS stay alive as hidden buttons; menu actions `.click()` them and `_ploc_sync_advanced_menu` mirrors text+enabled on `aboutToShow` (all gating/count labels flow through). **NEW hands-free "Auto re-anchor mosaic":** the manual re-anchor's live click auto-saves a ~¼-frame feature patch + its patch-centre abs µm + capture µm/px (`SupportClasses/ReanchorFeatureStore.py`, per plate key; position kept in the MAP frame — shifted by every `_ploc_apply_global_translation` and warped by the 2+ re-register); `_ploc_auto_reanchor` → `_AutoReanchorWorker(QThread)` (poller-suspended, `safe_travel_to` the stored position retract-first, `capture_fresh_frame`, patch rescaled by stored/live µm/px, `VisionDetector.find_template` TM_CCOEFF_NORMED conf-gate 0.5, rotation-aware `pixel_to_stage_offset`) → GUI applies `E = actual − stored` via the shared translation (mosaic+wells+feature move together). Gates: XY, Safe-Z-when-ZP, camera+µm/px, feature saved, map exists. **New "Rosettes" tab** (index 3, after Plate Location; `_zauto_tab_index` 3→4 — only hard-coded index): rosette-well picker ("(scanned)" annotated) + "Scan rosette well" (`_ploc_scan_single_well(name, True)` — factored out of `_ploc_scan_well`) + **"Map sub-wells from saved scan…"** (re-opens the mapping from the persisted `plate#well` mosaic + stored shift/µm-px — no re-scan/motion) + status + own live feed started by `_on_workflow_tab_changed`. **Single-well mosaics overlay the plate mosaic:** `MosaicStore.composite_with_wells` pastes each `plate#well` image at its extent (proportional crop when clipped); "Well scans" toggles (default ON) on Plate Location + Jog; **purity guard** — `_ploc_overlay_img` stays the PLAIN plate image (what `_ploc_shift_mosaic_by` re-persists), the composite exists only in the pushed pixmap; `_ploc_shift_mosaic_by` also translates every well-entry extent (metadata-only) so everything moves as one. **"Mosaic + well" = circles only:** `JogWorkspaceView` overlay mode draws NO plate body and outline-only rings (green cal / yellow approx / blue current) for every entry INCLUDING sub-wells (`_well_radius_um(name)` resolves per-well diameters); "well" mode byte-identical. Implemented alongside a concurrent session's calibration.py edits (Pump Compliance tab) — reconciled. **Needs real-HW/GUI verification on ME3B V1** (scan→auto-mapping main-wells-only lands correctly; Rosettes tab sub-wells land correctly — the shift bug fix; manual re-anchor saves the feature → auto re-anchor travels/matches/shifts; well-scan overlay + circles-only view). Tests: `tests/test_v75x_rosette_tab_auto_reanchor.py` (43); plate-mosaic/single-well-reregister/reanchor-camera/mosaic-remap/click-rim suites green (1 standing documented CV failure `test_real_24_well_mosaic`). **Addendum (2026-07-09, operator follow-ups):** Mosaic buttons now a VERTICAL single-column list with **"Map wells…" promoted onto it** (5 buttons; no longer hidden/menu-mirrored); **rosette PARENTS always fit with the plate's NORMAL well diameter** (dialog `_fit_diameter_mm`: no-WellInfo parent → `_main_well_diameter_mm()` = median non-sub-well Ø — was the 3 mm unknown fallback on custom plates where `well_diameter`=0.0); new Advanced **"Load mosaic from another plate…"** (`MosaicStore.list_plate_keys` + `_ploc_copy_mosaic_key` copies image/extent/scale/shift + `src#well` scans + mapping); and **the last good well mapping is stored WITH the mosaic** (`MosaicStore.set_wells/get_wells` meta `wells_um`, written on Map-wells confirm + re-derive; `save()` deliberately drops it on a fresh scan, so `_ploc_shift_mosaic_by` re-stores it SHIFTED and the copy helper carries it; `_ploc_apply_stored_mosaic_wells` auto-applies on load, names filtered vs `main_well_names`). Pre-addendum mosaics have no stored mapping — run Map wells… once to seed. |
| `MEBP_v75x_SINGLE_WELL_MOSAIC_AND_GUIDED_MAPPING.md` | v7.5.x three operator requests on Calibration → Plate Location. **(1) Single-well mosaics first-class:** "Scan well…" generalized to ANY well (`_ploc_scan_well`; rosette parents keep the sub-well flow, plain wells get a centre pick; new `_ploc_well_scan_bounds` + `_SingleWell` adapter); the composite now **persists** under `MosaicStore` key `"{plate_key}#{well}"` (`_ploc_apply_single_well_mosaic`; new store helpers `list_well_keys`/`has_well`; never clobbers the plate mosaic — verified nothing enumerates keys); rosette **sub-well centres AND the parent centre** merge into `_calibrated_positions` (centroid fallback). **(2) Guided, orientation-aware Map-wells dialog** (`mosaic_well_mapping_dialog.py` reworked): the mosaic is displayed in the PLATE orientation via a **pure `QGraphicsView.rotate(180)`** when `plate_flip_180` (scene coords/back-projection **byte-identical** — locked by a flip-invariance test; plate flow = confirm 3 corners → `solve_affine_3` autofill → per-well refine (auto-detect pre-places → straight to refine); **rosette flow** = place the pattern CENTRE once → sub-wells auto-place at `centre + (predicted_µm − centroid)×scale` (rotation + plate_axis_sign already baked in) → drag refine. **(3) "Re-register from scanned wells (N)"**: each pick records a session-only anchor `{measured, map-at-pick, merged pins}`; 1 anchor → `E = measured − map` via `_ploc_apply_global_translation(E, pin=)` (wells + markers + warp + mosaic overlay together); 2+ → `register_from_template` similarity/affine + plausibility gate + explicit-positions commit (`_plate_warp=None`, `_save_calibration()`) + mosaic follows the **translation component only** (`_ploc_shift_mosaic_for_warp`, labelled). **Self-review fixes:** anchor `merged` **pins** prevent double-correcting already-measured centres (pins folded into the warp rebuild → reload-safe); scan-start gates now snapshot+clear+re-arm the single-well flags (stale flags mis-routed a later full scan — pre-existing); anchors invalidated by ANY map-moving op (re-anchor/quick-reregister/re-derive/plate switch); **pre-existing bug fixed**: `_ploc_quick_reregister`'s `not self._warp_is_plausible(warp)` never fired (returns `tuple[bool,str]`) — now unpacked. **Needs real-HW/GUI verification on ME3B V1** (mosaic displays plate-oriented in Map wells; guided confirm in both modes; rosette centre→sub-wells `A1.a…` navigable; single-well mosaics persist + "(scanned)" annotation; 1-anchor re-register shifts mosaic+wells with the scanned well pinned at measured; 2–3 anchors upgrade the fit). Tests: `tests/test_v75x_single_well_mosaic_reregister.py` (26, incl. flip-invariance + double-correction regressions) + corners test updated in `test_v75x_plate_mosaic.py`; affected suites 220/221 (the 1 fail is the documented pre-existing CV `test_real_24_well_mosaic`). |
| `MEBP_v75x_WELL_TYPE_PRESETS.md` | v7.5.x **well-type presets for rosette wells** (operator: when adding a well to a rosette, pick a standard vessel — e.g. a **0.1 mL PCR tube** — instead of typing raw geometry; build out custom presets over time). The needle-clearance mechanism the request describes ALREADY exists (v7.4.8): a sub-well's **rim height** (how far the tube top sits above the plate) flows `Well.rim_height_mm` → `WellPlate.max_rim_height_mm` → `app.py::_update_insert_clearance` (`plate_top_z + max_rim + 3 mm margin`) → `StageController.set_min_travel_z` → floors every `safe_travel_to`/`ensure_retracted_to` retract (polarity-safe) so the needle clears the tallest tube before travelling over/into it — **that safety path is unchanged**. This adds a preset **library** to set that geometry in one click. **Decisions (operator):** full preset library (builtin + user-savable, builtin/user shadow-by-id) · single opening diameter (taper deferred) · reuse the existing rim-above-top semantics (no clearance-math change). **New `SupportClasses/WellTypeStore.py`** — pure-JSON `WellType` (id, display_name, `diameter_mm`, `well_depth_mm`, `rim_height_mm`, optional `ink_z_mm`/`volume_uL`, `builtin`) + `WellTypeStore` (`reload`/`get`/`all`/`save_user`/`delete_user`, atomic writes, lazy `get_store()` singleton), a close mirror of `PlateTypeStore`; dirs `config/hardware/well_types/{builtin,user}` (user shadows builtin by id). Ships **`builtin/pcr-tube-0.1ml.json`** (Ø 6, depth 20, rim 20, 100 µL; rim is a tunable guess). **Model:** `PlateDesign.Well.well_type_id: Optional[str]` (design-time link to the preset), conditional-serialized in `_entity_to_dict` (emit only when set → byte-identical legacy) + read in `_entity_from_dict`; `WellInfo`/`compile()` untouched (runtime uses the stamped geometry). **GUI** (`gui/pages/hardware/plate_designer.py`, `_build_well_card` rosette block, `_edit_context is not None`): a **Well type** `QComboBox` (`(custom)` + all types) + **Save as well type…** + **Delete** (user types only); handlers `_apply_well_type` (stamps diameter/depth/rim, and ink_z only when the type prescribes one; `None`=custom clears the link keeping geometry; scene redraw + `QTimer.singleShot(0, …)` deferred panel rebuild to avoid deleting the combo mid-signal), `_save_current_as_well_type`, `_delete_selected_well_type`. **Needs real-HW/GUI verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_well_type_presets.py` (18); rosette-flatten/plate-types/plate-designer regression (80) green. |
| `MEBP_v75x_SKETCH_OVERLAP_SEQUENCE_AND_NEEDLE_MODE.md` | v7.5.x three operator-requested **Print Builder → Sketch** additions. **(A) Closed-loop over-closure toggle:** `SketchShape.overlap_closure` (per-shape, closed outlines only; conditional serialize → legacy-identical) — the compiler continues the printed path PAST the seam by ~the needle **outer radius** (`od/2`, ½-bead fallback) via new `extend_closed_path(path, overshoot)` (appended per pass AFTER the weld-flip; ≤ one lap; never re-closes — intentionally ends past the seam), so the loop fully closes (otherwise the needle pushes ink aside on re-entry and it doesn't close). No-op on open/filled shapes. **(B) Print-sequence panel:** new `plan_print_sections(sketch, needle, single_needle)` returns the ordered list of colour-coded continuous **sections** (maximal runs printed as one bead) + the **moves** between them, MIRRORING the compiler grouping exactly (first-pass weld + far-end flip; last-pass end + overshoot as the exit; `break_before` = `move`/`ink_change`/`layer`); groups by OBJECT (layer 0). A persistent right-panel card renders it (section rows coloured by the pump→ink from `_channel_info` built from `config.pumps`, click→`canvas.select_indices`; break rows; summary). **(C) Single- vs multi-needle mode:** `Sketch.single_needle` (tri-state: None=AUTO→`needle.num_channels<=1`, else explicit; `is_single_needle(needle)`). Operator-chosen semantics (AskUserQuestion): a channel/ink change breaks the bead **only in single-needle mode** (the compiler tracks `prev_print_pump` — set on printing passes only — and refuses to weld across a channel change; move-only `no_print` passes never gate); multi keeps channels welded (legacy). `optimize_print_order` gained `needle`/`single_needle` (single mode groups same-channel shapes to cut ink swaps). Auto-detect checkbox on the sequence card, overridable. **Panel==compiled trajectory in both modes.** Latent fix: the page iterated `config.pumps` as a list (it's a dict) → never found a syringe; now dict-safe. **Adversarially reviewed** (4-dim workflow → skeptic verify: 5 confirmed / 1 rejected): fixed `prev_print_pump` set by move-only passes (HIGH), plan endpoint ignoring overlap overshoot (HIGH) + thick-outline last-pass end (MEDIUM); documented intra-object multi-pass lifts + odd-layer overlap direction as intentional layer-0/per-object approximations; rejected "extend_closed_path doesn't re-close" (intentional). **Needs GUI verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_sketch_overlap_sequence_needle_mode.py` (31); sketch + print-library/seam/quick-print/extrusion suites green (288). |
| `MEBP_v75x_CONTEXT_PANEL_PILLS_AND_CUSTOM_SECTIONS.md` | v7.5.x **left context panel becomes a pill-picker host + a composable Custom panel, and the "jog panel sometimes disappears in a workflow" bug is fixed** (operator). **Bug root cause:** the left box's **visibility** (`_navigate_to` → `UIFunctions.toggleLeftBox`, an *animation toggle* keyed on `isVisible()`) and its **content** (`_update_mode_context` from `sub_page_changed`, which never touched visibility) were decided by two paths that don't both run on every transition; `WorkflowsModePage` isn't a `ModePage`, so `_navigate_to` pointed the stack at a *placeholder* and the jog panel only mounted on a later `sub_page_changed` → order-dependent shown-but-empty / content-but-hidden. **Fix:** a **single global `ContextPanelHost`** (`gui/widgets/context_panel_host.py`) replaces the per-page left `_context_stack`, and **one `MainWindow._refresh_left_context()`** (called from BOTH `_navigate_to` and `_on_mode_sub_page_changed`; `_update_mode_context` deleted) mounts the current page's `get_context_widget()` in the host's native slot (reused `QScrollArea` wrappers keyed by widget id — never `deleteLater`) AND sets visibility **explicitly** (no toggle; exception in a delegate → treated as no-native, never vanishes). **Feature (operator chose view-picker + shared scope):** the host has a `SegmentedPillBar` — **(Jog)** = the page's native context widget, **(Custom)** = a shared `CustomContextPanel` (`gui/widgets/custom_context_panel.py`) the operator composes from **sections/cards** via `＋ Add section`: **live camera** (`CameraFeedView`; safe-start on show, never stops the shared feed), **syringe overview** (`PumpRack`, fill-dict from `jog_control._refresh_pump_panel`; ink accent `.color`), **positions** (new light `PositionReadoutCard` reusing `PositionBar` + `_update_position_displays` read logic; Z→user frame, pump→µL), **jog** (`StandardJogContextPanel`), **hardware info** — registry in `gui/widgets/context_sections.py` (`SectionContext` + `register_section`/`catalog`/`build_section`; each best-effort → a broken builder shows an error card). Layout is a **single shared, persisted, machine-level** thing — `SupportClasses/ContextPanelLayoutStore.py` (`config/context_panel_layout.json`; atomic tmp+`os.replace`, listeners, env `MEBP_CONTEXT_PANEL_DIR`, `known_types` prune) → same cards everywhere the box appears, survives restart. Live `on_status_update`/`on_motion_tick` forwarded app → host → Custom sections (native jog panel stays page-ticked → no double-drive). **Scope:** `in_scope = native context is not None` = exactly "everywhere the left box already appears" (Jog / Calibration / jog-capable Workflow tiles); Print Builder / Full Print inner / Fluorescence / Common Print Settings / picker return `None` and keep no box (unchanged) — so the `full_print` carve-out / `current_workflow_id()` from the draft plan proved unnecessary. Host persists the **requested** pill (`context_panel.active_view`) distinct from the *displayed* view so an availability-driven fallback can't clobber a saved "Jog" at startup; `context_panel.collapsed` remembers manual collapse. Close-x + top-bar toggle → `_toggle_left_context`; host added to `_propagate_hardware_config` fan-out. **Needs GUI verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_context_panel.py` (20; incl. `_refresh_left_context` invoked unbound against a fake `self` — full MainWindow boot is intractable headless); full-print/jog-nav/needle-location/plate-z-autocal/plate-location baselines green (121). |
| `MEBP_v75x_SKETCH_START_POINT_AND_PATH_OPTIMIZE.md` | v7.5.x two operator-requested **Print Builder → Sketch** additions for **print continuity** ("a shape is placed down and the start/stop location is standard … not good for print continuity"). **(1) Manual per-shape print start/stop with snap-to-lines:** new `SketchShape.start_point: tuple\|None` (conditional serialize → byte-identical legacy when `None`) — a world-mm anchor for WHERE a shape begins printing. Compiler helper `reorder_path_to_start(path, start_xy, closed)` (open → reverse to the nearer endpoint; closed → roll the ring to the nearest vertex + re-close; deposited geometry unchanged, only the entry/exit/seam moves); `_shape_paths` is now a thin wrapper over `_shape_paths_raw` that reorders each **unfilled outline** pass to the start point (fills / `region` / `travel` never reordered). Canvas gains a **draggable green start marker** (`_effective_start_world`/`_resolve_start_on_shape`/`_start_marker_screen`/`_start_handle_at`/`_draw_start_marker`; offset outward from the shape so it never collides with the blue resize handles; hit-tested BEFORE resize handles); dragging it reuses the existing `_snap(raw, exclude=selected)` → **snaps to existing lines/vertices**, stored via a new `_apply_resize` `"start"` case; `clear_start_point`. Page shows a "Print start: custom/default" row + hint + "Reset start point" for outline shapes. A shape can now start exactly where the previous one ended → the existing compiler **weld** machinery fuses them (no pen-up). **(2) Auto path-optimize postprocess (operator chose FULL reorder):** `optimize_print_order(sketch)` (pure, returns a copy) partitions the shape list at user-placed `travel` retract points (**fixed breaks**) and per segment runs a **greedy nearest-neighbour with all-seed restarts** (≤ 14 shapes), choosing each shape's entry orientation (rotate loop / flip line) and stamping `start_point` to chain them into the fewest pen-up travels; preserves all print params. Canvas `optimize()` (undoable) + a "✨ Optimize print path" button on the Print-parameters card reporting "N → M travel(s)" via `count_discontinuities` (counts lift-to-max-Z groups − 1 — the `n_lifts` ground truth; immune to the zero-length coincident weld-node segment that fooled a first pump-flat-run metric). **Fills/regions kept fixed** in the optimizer v1 (ordered, not reoriented). Design-time only (no motion); embedded vector `Sketch` (Library "Edit in Sketch") round-trips the new field. **Needs GUI verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_sketch_start_point_and_optimize.py` (33); existing sketch suites (154) + print-library/seam/quick-print-travel (40) green. |
| `MEBP_v75x_REANCHOR_MOSAIC_AND_CAMERA_ORIENTATION.md` | v7.5.x two operator-requested plate-calibration changes. **(1) Re-anchor moves the mosaic + wells as ONE unit, no-travel.** The mosaic is scanned once per plate TYPE; when the plate is remounted the whole map is offset. "Re-anchor map" previously shifted only the **well centres**, leaving the **mosaic image** behind. Now `CalibrationPage._ploc_apply_global_translation` also calls new `_ploc_shift_mosaic_by(ex,ey)` (extracted from `_ploc_store_manual_align`, which is refactored to reuse it) — shifts the cached overlay extent, persists to `MosaicStore` (before the `calibration_data_changed` emit → the Jog page re-reads the shifted extent), re-pushes via `_ploc_set_overlay_image` (idempotent: resets the view user-shift + align sliders). Re-anchor is now **NO-TRAVEL**: `_ploc_reanchor_live_click` (step 1) captures the feature's true absolute µm from the LIVE click (`get_xy_position(cached=False)` + `pixel_to_stage_offset`), `_ploc_reanchor_overview` (step 2) reads the MAP position from an overview click (well-snap `_predict_well_xy` or Free-select zero-ref+zero), `E = live − map` (same sign as the old `feature_abs − target`), `_ploc_safe_goto` NO LONGER called, Safe-Z gate dropped (no motion); overview click handlers gate on `_ploc_reanchor_stage` so a stray click can't pollute the run queue. Robust to an arbitrarily large remount offset (the old travel-first flow only worked when the offset was < ~½ FOV). **(2) Calibrated camera-vs-stage rotation applied to the click mapping.** `CameraManager.pixel_to_stage_offset` ignored the camera's mount rotation (identity: image-right=+X, down=+Y) → a rotated microscope camera drove live-view clicks the wrong way. It now applies the calibrated `rotation_deg` as `(dx_um,dy_um)=µm/px·R(θ)·(dx_px,dy_px)` (standard CCW `R(θ)`); **at θ=0/None it reduces EXACTLY to the legacy identity** (no regression for aligned/uncalibrated cameras). The sign is DERIVED from `PixelCalibrationDialog.plus_column_direction_deg`'s documented model (`p=−scale·R(α)·m`, stored `θ=−α`, so click→stage inverse is `µm/px·R(θ)·p`) and locked by a round-trip test. **Only the click mapping changes** — the displayed feed, the raw frames, and the `MosaicBuilder` build (which places tiles by stage position with NO per-frame rotation, so a capture-level flip would scramble it) are all untouched; changing orientation does NOT invalidate a scanned mosaic. Reuses the existing measurement/storage/restore infra: a new **"Calibrate orientation (vs stage)…"** button + "Camera rotation vs stage: θ°" readout on the `objective_calibration_card` measures rotation via `PixelCalibrationDialog` (no µm/px recal), pushes `set_rotation_deg` live, persists per camera identity via new `CameraCalibrationStore.set_rotation`/`get_rotation` (rotation-only write preserving `um_per_px`/`image_correction`/`hw_controls` siblings) + syncs the fresh rotation into EVERY objective with a µm/px cal (mount rotation is a camera property, not per-objective); `hardware_setup._restore_calibration_for_slot` now restores `rotation_deg` **independent of µm/px**. **Adversarially reviewed** (4-dim workflow → independent verify): rotation-math + re-anchor-flow clean; 3 confirmed defects fixed — malformed-extent guard in `_ploc_shift_mosaic_by` (IndexError → logged no-op), the all-objectives rotation sync above (an objective swap could push a STALE per-objective rotation over the fresh one), and pre-existing `ObjectiveCalibrationStore.set_calibration` no longer drops a stored `rotation_deg` on a µm/px-only update. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1** (calibrate orientation → plausible angle; live-view click maps the right way — else flip the one-line `R(θ)` sign; re-anchor a remounted plate → mosaic + wells shift together on Plate Location + Jog, no stage motion, persists on restart). Tests: `tests/test_v75x_reanchor_mosaic_and_camera_orientation.py` (21, incl. the sign-lock round-trip); objective-cal/camera-rotation/camera-cal-store (65) + image-correction/mosaic-orientation-remap/plate-orientation/plate-location-click-rim green; plate-mosaic 115/116 (the 1 fail is the documented pre-existing CV `test_real_24_well_mosaic`). |
| `MEBP_v75x_SKETCH_RETRACT_POINT_AND_BACKTRACE.md` | v7.5.x two operator-requested **Print Builder → Sketch** additions. **(1) Colour-coded retract-&-move point** ("a color coded point … that retracts and moves the needle"): new `"travel"` shape kind — a placeable pen-up marker (pink diamond + up-chevron, `Tool.TRAVEL`, `TRAVEL_HEX`). The compiler emits `lift_out(z_travel)` → move to its `cx,cy` at the retracted travel Z (deposits nothing, stays up); it **splits the toolpath into runs**. **(2) Back-trace any path** ("take the continuous collection of lines between the retract points and move the needle back through with a user defined offset" + follow-up "also … in the plane"): retrace a run **reversed**, offset in **Z-height** AND **in-plane** (perpendicular parallel curve), with an **extrude-on-return toggle** (2nd bead / move-only). **Model:** `SketchShape` gains `z_offset_mm` (per-shape print-height offset) + `no_print` (move-only), both serialized (defaults → byte-identical legacy). **Compiler:** per-shape print Z = `max(0, layer_z + z_offset_mm)` (floored at the plate bottom so a negative offset can't punch through), `do_print = not no_print`, `z_travel` clears the tallest offset. New module fns `offset_polyline(pts, distance, closed)` (per-vertex averaged-normal parallel offset, consecutive-duplicate-safe, clamped miter) + `backtrace_shape(shape, *, z_offset, xy_offset, print_on_return)` (reversed + offset copy; lines/polys/regions reverse points + in-plane offset, circle/ellipse/rect grow by the offset; accumulated Z clamped ±40 mm so chained back-traces can't run away). **Canvas** (`sketch_canvas.py`): `_place_travel`, `_run_indices(seed)` (maximal printing run bounded by travel points), `backtrace_run(seed, z_off, xy_off, print_on_return)` inserts reversed copies right after the run; travel cases in draw/hit/extent/move/transform/snap; `fit_view` scale clamped to 200 (tiny-span guard). **Page** (`print_builder_sketch.py`): retract tool button, travel-point props card, `_build_backtrace_card` (Z / in-plane offset spins + "Extrude on return" checkbox + button) shown for a single non-travel selection; `_backtrace_selected` confirms on a negative Z offset (collision nudge). **Adversarially reviewed** (4-dim workflow → verify, 21/38 confirmed): 6 fixes (chained-Z clamp, offset-polyline dedup, negative-Z confirm, fit-view zoom clamp, explicit travel no-ops); two HIGH flags proven non-bugs + locked with tests (multi-layer serpentine only flips travel *direction* not deposited geometry; travel-as-first-shape starts retracted at z_travel, and the `build_well_plate_job` preamble retracts anyway). **Needs GUI verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_sketch_retract_and_backtrace.py` (43); sketch/print-library/quick-print/multi-object-seam/print-setup-routine suites green (193). |
| `MEBP_v75x_EXTRUSION_VOLUME_CALC_AUDIT.md` | v7.5.x **audit + fixes for "27 G needs ~7× extrusion to print anything"** (operator asked for a full print-volume calculation check). **Verdict: the core arithmetic is correct — no units bug / hidden factor.** The whole Quick Print chain deposits exactly `bore_area × modifier` µL per mm (`flow = pct·area·xy_max·mod`, `speed = pct·xy_max` → `vol/mm = flow/speed = area·mod`; `move_pump_uL` µL→plunger-mm is clean; the Hagen–Poiseuille flow ceiling for a 27 G at water is ~376 µL/s, far above any print flow, so the clamp isn't limiting). Needing a multiplier is BY DESIGN — the 1× model = a filament the exact diameter of the bore = the theoretical minimum; a visible/spreading bead + prime + compliance need more. Four findings compounded it; operator chose to fix **A + C + E** (D folded into A). **(C) drop-gate under-extrusion (real defect):** `PrintManager._execute_print_path` skipped any segment whose `volume_uL ≤ 0.001` (at 27 G 1× = every segment < 0.029 mm) and `ZPStage.move_relative` drops `<1e-4 mm` plunger deltas — NEITHER accumulated → finely-sampled paths lost volume, worst at low extrusion (→ operator over-cranks the modifier). Fix: accumulate a `_pending_pump_uL` residual across segments, emit once it crosses `_PATH_PUMP_EMIT_MIN_UL` (0.001 µL), flush the tail at path end; pacing/logging still use the nominal per-segment volume (timing unchanged). **(A) two inconsistent bead models unified:** the Sketch compiler used `id_mm × layer_height × mult` (rectangle, layer-height-dependent) while FlowPhysics/GeometryEngine/Quick Print use `π·(id/2)² × mult` (bore area) — CLAUDE.md F-1 claimed "ONE bead model everywhere" but Sketch was never migrated. `SketchTrajectory.compile_to_trajectory` now uses the canonical **bore-area × mult** when a needle is supplied (falls back to `bead × layer_height × mult` only when no needle); its shaded thickness band stays a display WIDTH (`id × mult`) — width vs area differ by design, same as Quick Print. **(D) stale GeometryEngine module docstring** ("flow_rate = travel_speed × needle_OD × layer_height") updated to the bore-area model. **(E) modifier had no effect on the parametric/trajectory path:** `PrintObject.extrusion_modifier` existed but `generate_object_trajectory` never read it (called `compute_pump_positions`/`compute_total_volume` at 1.0) and there was no UI → Print Builder parametric objects / Full Print ran locked at 1×. Fix: `generate_object_trajectory` reads `obj.extrusion_modifier` and threads it through the 11 internal builders → the volume functions (all new params default 1.0 = byte-identical legacy; explicit `outer_ring_volume_uL` stays an absolute target since the modifier cancels in its ratio); a new **"Extrusion ×"** spin in the Print Builder object designer captured into `params["extrusion_modifier"]`, lifted onto the field in `_build_print_object` + the collection-commit `PrintObject`. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1** (re-tune the 27 G multiplier — fine-path drops fixed + parametric path now honors the modifier, so it should need a smaller value). Tests: `tests/test_v75x_extrusion_volume_calc.py` (14) + updated `test_v75x_sketch_extrusion_thickness.py`; extrusion/sketch/quick-print/seam/planner-barrier/print-setup/full-print/library suites green (294). Pre-existing unrelated failures: `test_v73_trajectory_planner.TestFullPlanGeneration` (6, MagicMock `<` in `PrintTrajectoryPlanner._move_z`, untouched by this change). |
| `MEBP_v75x_JOG_TRAVEL_OFF_GUI_THREAD.md` | v7.5.x **fix — "program freezes when I jog to another well with the needle DOWN, but not if I hit Move-to-Safe-Z first"** (operator; suspected a safety-check bug). **Root cause (4-lens investigation + adversarial verify + `logs/app.log`):** NOT a broken safety check and NOT a deadlock — the Jog page's click-to-travel handlers called `StageController.safe_travel_to(...)` **synchronously on the Qt GUI thread**; step-1 Z-retract-and-confirm (`flush_moves` M400 + `wait_for_z_arrival`) takes **11–14 s** on ME3B V1 (up to ~60–120 s on a stall), freezing the event loop the whole time. "Move to Safe Z" first is fire-and-forget (`move_z_absolute` returns instantly), so the subsequent click's step-1 retract is a fast no-op → no freeze. The workflow pages (Quick Print pre-position, pick-&-place executors) already ran `safe_travel_to` off-thread; the Jog page + the workflow-page CLICK handlers were the outliers. **Fix (operator chose full + serial hardening):** new reusable `gui/widgets/safe_travel_worker.py::SafeTravelWorker(QObject)` runs `safe_travel_to` on a daemon thread, marshals `finished(bool)` back via a queued Signal, **busy-guard** so re-clicks are ignored (not queued behind each other on the shared serial channel). Wired into all 5 click sites: `jog_control` (workspace click + right-click fast-travel; + WaitCursor cue), `standard_jog_context._absolute_goto` (Absolute Go-To; Go button → "Travelling…"), and `spheroid_pickup`/`cell_targeting`/`cell_labeling` (both click handlers). Retract-before-XY safety **unchanged** (same `safe_travel_to` args; only *where the blocking wait runs* changed). **Serial hardening:** `ZPStage._read_until_ok` gains an absolute wall-clock cap `READ_OK_HARD_CAP_S=180 s` (`hard_deadline = now + max(ok_timeout, cap)`, top-of-loop check → outcome `hard_timeout`) so a never-completing move — whose Marlin `busy` keep-alives used to reset the wait window forever — fails after a generous ceiling instead of hanging the worker thread (and every ZP thread behind `_serial_lock`) indefinitely; `flush_moves` (own bounded loop) unaffected. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_jog_travel_off_gui_thread.py` (8) + updated `test_v75x_z_retract_before_xy_travel.py` (join the worker thread); jog-nav/workflow-settings/ZP-serial/gentle-Z/safe-Z/spheroid/cell-targeting/cell-labeling/stress green. Pre-existing unrelated failure: `test_v75x_zp_auto_reconnect_and_fast_z::test_no_z_max_keeps_defaults` (uncommitted `get_max_z_feedrate_mm_min` WIP in StageController.py, not this change). |

| `MEBP_v75x_SPHEROID_DISENGAGE_AND_SINK_TIMING.md` | v7.5.x **Spheroid Pick & Place: optional disengagement extra-aspirate + calibrated sink-timing model + guided calibration** (operator: a spheroid rises up the bore then SINKS; if it sinks before the needle leaves the well it's lost, if aspirated too far up it gets stuck, and at placement they want it just at the tip for minimal excess; also sometimes needs extra flow to pop it off the glass). **Model:** `height_mm = V_uL / bore_area_mm2` (bore = `NeedleSpec.cross_section_area_mm2`). Calibration builds a **timing curve `t_sink(lift)`** via a **cumulative staircase** (aspirate increasing volumes on one test spheroid; operator clicks "Visible at tip" each time it sinks back → sample `(lift=ΔV/area, sink_s)`). At run time (per move) the executor **inverts** the curve so `sink_time ≈ travel_time + margin`, sizing the aspirate to `max(carrier, timing_vol)` (**floor at the sphere-capture volume**) and waiting only the leftover at the destination — the spheroid stays lifted through the in-well retract, isn't lifted more than needed, and arrives at the tip for a **small release dispense** (needle keeps the rest; Post-clean clears the accumulation). **Disengagement** = an extra held aspirate applied as the **fast leading portion** of the total. **New:** `SupportClasses/SpheroidSinkCalibrationStore.py` (atomic-JSON singleton + standalone `SinkCurve` with monotone/clamped `lift_for_time`/`time_for_lift`; needle-independent — volume↔lift uses the *current* bore area); `SpheroidPickupConfig` fields (`disengage_enabled/volume_uL/rate_uL_s`, `sink_timing_enabled`, `travel_margin_s`, `release_enabled/volume_uL`) + `to_dict`; `PickPlaceExecutor` gains `bore_area_mm2`/`sink_curve` + `_estimate_travel_time_s` (XY dist ÷ measured/limit XY speed + Z retract/lower + overhead; div-0 safe) + `_planned_aspirate_uL`; `_execute_spheroid_pickup` rewritten. **GUI** (`spheroid_pickup_workflow.py`): "Sink timing & disengage (optional)" settings section + `_current_config`/`_on_start` wiring & gates (needle Ø + calibrated curve required when sink timing on) + `_bore_area_mm2()` + calibration-status label + `hideEvent` hook. **New dialog** `gui/pages/workflows/spheroid_sink_calibration.py::SinkDisengageCalibrationDialog` (modeless; live microscope; worker/bridge; staircase + disengage-test panels; every worker/Abort/hide ends at Safe Z; ZP-connected/Safe-Z/plate-bottom/bore-area preflight gates). **All new behavior OFF by default → byte-identical legacy sequence.** Aspirate=`−`/dispense=`+`, spheroid moves keep `relieve=False`. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_spheroid_disengage_and_sink_timing.py` (21); spheroid-pick-place-z / workflow-settings-popout / pump-settle / cell-targeting / cell-labeling / picker-scaling suites green (142+). |
| `MEBP_v75x_PUMP_ENVELOPE_RESTORE_FROM_SETUP.md` | v7.5.x **fix — on restart a calibrated pump's LOCATION restores but its soft-limit MIN/MAX do not** (operator). On ME3B V3, P2 came up `[0, 30]` instead of the calibrated `[-30, 0]`. **Root cause:** the envelope had two persisted sources that drifted — `device_profile.pump_setup` (captured raw extremes, authoritative: P2 `raw_dispensed=0, raw_aspirated=-30` ⇒ `[-30,0]`) vs the separate `safety_limits.p2_min/max` mirror (stale, sign-flipped `[0,30]`). At startup `apply_pump_convention` restored the **datum + direction sign** from `pump_setup` but **not the envelope** (deferred to "the safety_limits section separately"), and `set_hardware_config`'s `update_from_hardware_config(skip_pumps=[…])` clobber-guard then **preserved** the stale mirror (it only blocks the syringe-stroke estimate; it never re-derives the calibrated envelope) → calibration owned datum+direction but not the envelope it captured. Secondary re-clobber: `app.py::_apply_and_save_after_restore` mirrors persisted `safety_limits` back into the live envelope on restore-prompt accept. **Fix:** `StageController.apply_pump_convention` now **re-derives the soft-limit envelope from the captured extremes** (`lo,hi = min/max(rd,ra)`, exact extremes/no margin, matching `apply_pump_setup`) so datum+direction+envelope all come from the one authoritative `pump_setup` on every boot (self-heals future drift); plus corrected the stale on-disk `safety_limits.p2` `[0,30]`→`[-30,0]` in `settings.json` + `config/hardware/devices/ME3B V3.json` so every source agrees (closes the `_apply_and_save_after_restore` hole). Uncalibrated pumps (no `pump_setup`) unaffected — still recomputed from the syringe stroke each boot. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V3.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_pump_plunger_setup.py` (25, +`test_envelope_restored_overrides_stale_mirror` + envelope assertion in `test_restore_sign_and_datum`); pump-plunger/common-axis-speed/zp-envelope-absolute/jog-pump-fill suites green (62). |
| `MEBP_v75x_MOSAIC_MEMORY_AND_OVERLAY_PERF.md` | v7.5.x **fix — mosaic capture crawls past ~300 tiles (RAM 80-90%) + app laggy after build** (`changes_needed.md` items 1 & 2). **Root cause:** `MosaicBuilder._records` retained EVERY raw frame (~2 MB → ~½ GB at 300 tiles) + the float64 `_composite`/`_weight_sum` (~190 MB) lived the whole scan → swap thrash → seconds/tile; and `JogWorkspaceView._paint_mosaic_overlay` re-scaled the ~18 MB mosaic pixmap with a 180° painter transform on EVERY paintEvent. **Fix:** new `MosaicBuilder(retain_frames=False)` frees each tile's frame right after `stitch_incremental` blends it (full-plate scan only — that path never re-blends; per-well `build_mosaic` + the calibration dialog `tile_images_px` keep `retain_frames=True`; `build_mosaic`/`_ensure_composite`/`tile_images_px` skip freed frames defensively); new `free_accumulators()` releases the float64 buffers post-scan keeping the uint8 display cache (called by `_MosaicScanWorker.run` after detection); the overlay now caches the scaled+rotated pixmap keyed by `(w,h,flip,source-id)` (pan/needle-motion reuse it; the manual nudge is size-invariant; invalidated on `set_mosaic_overlay`). A per-tile preview-emit throttle was prototyped + reverted (broke the one-preview-per-tile worker contract; the memory fixes alone resolve the slowdown). **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_mosaic_memory_and_overlay_perf.py` (8); plate-mosaic + orientation-remap green (only the documented pre-existing CV `test_real_24_well_mosaic` fails). |

The backend ceiling (`SafetyLimits.update_from_hardware_config`→`FlowPhysics.max_safe_flow_rate_uL_s`, water-ref viscosity → hard clamp in `move_pump_uL`), the `StageController` speed resolvers, and per-workflow pickup/deposit rate spinboxes already existed (user WIP). | `MEBP_v75x_PUMP_RELIEF_AND_BORE_BEAD_MODEL.md` | v7.5.x **two operator-confirmed changes from the workflow-move audit (`coding plans/Analyses/MEBP_WORKFLOW_MOVE_AUDIT.md`): F-1 one bead model + F-4 redesigned pressure relief.** **F-1 (bead model):** the deposited volume-per-mm is now **inner-bore cross-section × extrusion modifier** everywhere (the "thickness of the laid line"), replacing the legacy **outer-Ø × layer_height** in `FlowPhysics.extrusion_flow_rate` + `GeometryEngine.compute_pump_positions`/`compute_total_volume` (all gain a `modifier=1.0` kwarg; `layer_height` kept as a param but **no longer scales volume** — it is now only the Z step between stacked layers). Quick Print already used this model; this aligns the trajectory / Print-Builder path. `PrintObject.extrusion_modifier` field added (default 1.0, serialized) so the data model is ready — **per-object modifier UI in Print Builder is a tracked follow-up** (the trajectory path runs at modifier 1.0, byte-stable canonical bore model). `line_spacing` (fill raster pitch) deliberately left on `od_mm` (separate fill-density lever). **F-4 (pressure relief):** relief is now **% of each pump's syringe volume** (`HardwareConfig.pump_relief_percent`, default **1 %**, tunable ~0.001–5 %; per-pump µL = pct/100 × syringe vol) — replaces the old absolute `pump_relief_volume_uL` (legacy value discarded on load → default 1 %). Three independent **toggles, all default ON** (`pump_relief_on_pickup/deposit/quick_move`). `StageController` gains `pump_relief_percent()`/`pump_relief_uL(pump)`/`pump_relief_on_*()`; `move_pump_uL(relieve=True)` is now **direction-aware**: an ASPIRATE (pickup) → DISPENSE the relief back (bleed vacuum, existing); a DISPENSE (deposit) → ASPIRATE the relief back (**suck-back, stop drool**, new), each gated by its toggle. `PrintManager._print_pump_suckback` does the **deposit** suck-back (end of each `_execute_print_path`) and the **quick-move** suck-back (before each inter-object hop `MOVE_XY`; re-primed by the next object's prime) — the quick-move case also addresses `changes_needed.md` item 5. **Scope decision:** deposit/quick-move relief is **ink-printing only** — the volume-balanced spheroid/cell **micro-captures keep `relieve=False`** (a 1 %-of-syringe relief = ~2.5 µL would dwarf their nL volumes and break net-zero). **GUI:** Hardware Setup → Pump "Pump Timing" relief spin → **% of syringe** + 3 checkboxes (`_rebuild_config`/`_apply_config_to_ui`); the 4 prep-workflow "Common — Pump (global)" rows + the Common Print Settings page row swapped to `pump_relief_percent`; `CommonPrintSettings.GLOBAL_KEYS`/`DEFAULTS` updated. **F-2 follow-on:** the global 10 s settle was a residual-pressure workaround now superseded by this relief — operator can lower `pump_settle_time_s` (config, no code). **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1/V3.** Tests: new `tests/test_v75x_pump_relief_and_bead_model.py` (14: bore-area math, modifier scaling, layer-height-ignored, %→µL per pump, deposit/quick-move suck-back + toggle gating, migration); updated `test_v75x_pump_settle_and_prime_time.py` (relief now % + direction-aware) + `test_v75x_common_print_settings.py` (key rename); relief/settings/bead (71) + workflow-settings/4-workflow-pages/sketch (153) + print-path (62) + config-serialize (85) suites green. Pre-existing failures (confirmed by stashing): `test_v73_trajectory_planner.TestFullPlanGeneration` (6) + `test_v75x_pump_plunger_setup.TestDirectionOwnershipInMotion` (3, an uncommitted `MotionEstimator` change makes `move_pump_relative` read `_pos_poller` a `__new__`-partial test controller lacks). |

empty → meander fallback `path_segments=None`); consumes stashed `intra_well_hop_z_mm`/`line_move_*_speed_mm_s`/`travel_speed`. **NOTE (honest scope):** the seam fix lands on the **discrete** path (`_build_current_job`, the FALLBACK); the full print's DEFAULT executed path is **hybrid** (`_generate_print`→`PrintTrajectoryPlanner`+`PrintPlanOfAction`), whose in-well multi-object trajectory is separate + NOT changed here — a hybrid/trajectory seam pass is a deferred follow-up (keep `execution.mode`=\"hybrid\"; the trajectory planner has a known ZDIR=+1 gap on ME3B V1). **(3) Shared settings + import:** `set_common_print_settings` plumbed wrapper → `WizardPrintSetupPage` → legacy (reaches it via the existing `_fanout_common_print_settings`→workflows-mode fanout; globals already applied at execution via the controller). | `MEBP_v75x_JOG_PUMP_FILL_READOUT.md` | v7.5.x **fix — the Jog page syringe showed no current volume and never auto-updated** (operator). **Root cause:** the Jog pump rack (`gui/widgets/pump_rack.py` → `PumpColumn._refresh`) rendered `fc.ink_volume_uL` from the **static** `pump_cfg.fluid_column` — a layered oil/buffer/ink model only mutated by `aspirate_ink`/`dispense` **during a print/workflow**, never during manual jogging — so it displayed a frozen config value (usually `0.0 µL`). The ~300 ms status tick (`app.py::_tick`→`_update_status`→`page.on_status_update`→`_refresh_pump_panel`) *was* firing; it just refreshed a constant. Both halves of the complaint had this one cause. The Hardware **Control Panel** was already reframed in v7.5.x (`MEBP_v75x_PUMP_PLUNGER_CAL_AND_ASPIRATE_DISPENSE.md`) to show live fill via `controller.raw_to_pump_fill_uL`; the Jog rack was never migrated. **Fix (mirror the control panel):** new `PumpColumn.set_live_fill(*, fill_uL, capacity_uL, raw_mm, syringe, ink_spec, calibrated)` + `PumpRack.update_live_fills(fills)`; `JogControlPage._refresh_pump_panel` rewritten to derive each tick from `is_pump_plunger_calibrated`/`raw_to_pump_fill_uL`(RAW Marlin mm→µL)/`pump_capacity_uL` and feed the rack. **Calibrated** → fill µL (0=empty→capacity=full) + proportional bar + low-fill warning; **configured but un-calibrated** → live raw Marlin mm + "calibrate plunger for µL" hint; **no syringe** → "not configured". The barrel now renders a single fill level (no live oil/buffer/ink layering on the Jog page). `-0.0` at the empty datum normalised to `0.0` (genuine negatives kept). **Adversarial review (3 lenses→verify):** fixed (a) un-calibrated mm frame divergence — show raw `p_raw` to match the embedded control panel; (b) ink accent read the non-existent `InkSpec.display_color` (repo-wide misnomer) → use `.color`. Old `set_pump_loadout`/`update_from_workspace` path left as orphaned back-compat. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1** (jog a *calibrated* pump → µL readout + bar track the plunger live; un-calibrated → live raw mm + hint). Tests: `tests/test_v75x_jog_pump_fill_readout.py` (12); pump-plunger/jog-nav/axis-map/jog-direction/pump-settle suites green (123). |
| `MEBP_v75x_PLATE_TYPES_Z_OFFSETS_AND_MOSAIC.md` | v7.5.x **selectable plate TYPES on Hardware Setup → Plate: per-type Z offsets + auto-loading mosaics** (operator request). A well-count ships as different products (Corning glass-bottom, NEST plastic-bottom, generic) that share the XY grid but differ in **bottom material → different needle Z references**, and each has its **own full-plate mosaic**. **(1)** the automatic Z-needle offsets — **Plate-Bottom, Fast-Move/Safe, Max, AND Plate-Top Z** — now **inherit from the selected plate type** instead of the single global Device offset set (Replace stays manual); **(2)** each type's mosaic/template/well-training auto-loads on select. **Key lever:** `MosaicStore`/`PlateTemplateStore`/`WellTrainingStore` are all keyed by `active_plate_key`, and the reload triggers already fire on plate change — so a **distinct `active_plate_key` per type** makes the data auto-segregate through existing paths. A plate type is a **thin overlay on a base standard format**: geometry inherited (runtime `WellPlate.format` stays the base `int` → same-format guards/`PLATE_DEFINITIONS` unaffected), identity = the type id (`active_plate_key`), Z offsets `{top,bottom,safe,max}` mm below the needle-cam fiducial. **New `SupportClasses/PlateTypeStore.py`** (pure-JSON singleton, mirror of `MosaicStore`): `PlateType` dataclass; built-ins `config/hardware/plate_types/builtin/*.json` + user overrides `…/user/*.json` where **a user entry shadows the built-in by id** (the learn-loop writes a user override; built-in stays pristine). `HardwareConfig.plate_type_id` + `active_plate_key` precedence **type → name → format** (+ serialize + `validate()` dangling-id check). `WellPlate.load` resolves a type id → `from_format(base_format)` (+ optional `well_depth_mm` override; unknown id safe). `StageController` Z offsets gain **`max`** (`set_plate_z_offsets`/`estimate_plate_z_refs`→`plate_max_z`/`apply_z_convention`) + new `_apply_active_plate_type_offsets` pushes the active type's offsets in `set_hardware_config` (generic → keep device-profile fallback; Max Z still **never** written to `safety_limits.z_max`). Calibration: `_zoff_estimate_from_needle_cam` also fills Max Z; new `_apply_plate_type_z_estimates(force=False)` (taught-wins: fills only `None` refs, hooked at the end of `set_hardware_config` after `_load_calibration` so taught values survive boot ordering); **learn-loop button** `_zoff_save_offsets_to_plate_type` (offset = fiducial − user_z(ref) → user PlateType override). Mosaic/template/training keys now derive from `active_plate_key` (`_jog_plate_key`/`_ploc_plate_key`/sub-well key — fixes the base-int collision). **Two-step Format→Type card** above the Plate designer (`hardware_setup.py`): Format combo filters a Plate-type combo (Generic + products) + offsets readout; sets `plate_type_id`, drives the designer's base geometry, syncs on `set_hardware_config`; designer's own picker clears the type. Seeded built-ins: **6/24/96-well × {glass, plastic}** (z_offsets best-guess until taught). **Per-plate-key calibration:** the WHOLE taught calibration (XY taught wells / warp / affine / Z references / Z-plane / reference markers) is **archived per `active_plate_key`** (new `settings.calibration_by_plate`) and **auto-restored when a plate is selected**, so calibration *belongs to the plate* (each type keeps its own map). Dual-section design keeps the flat `settings.calibration` mirroring the ACTIVE plate (every legacy flat reader — `print_setup_legacy`/`app.py` snapshot restore — untouched); new `CalibrationPage` helpers `_calibration_key`/`_calibration_archive` (one-time migrates a legacy flat block under its `plate_format`)/`_reset_calibration_state`/`_activate_calibration_for_key`, `_save_calibration(key=)` dual-writes; a plate switch (`set_hardware_config` key change + in-page `_on_plate_changed`) flushes the OUTGOING plate's block (before the geometry rebuild) then restores the INCOMING plate's (or resets to blank → type-offset auto-fill takes over). **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1** (select Corning-24 → mosaic + Z guesses load; teach floor + Save offsets; teach plate A → switch to B → A's map leaves view → back to A → A's taught wells + Z return; switch to NEST-24 → different mosaic + guesses, shared geometry). Tests: `tests/test_v75x_plate_types.py` (32, +`TestPerPlateCalibration`); plate-mosaic (keys)/mosaic-orientation-remap/cal-z-envelope/last-known-calibration/plate-centering/orientation/printing-mode-calibrated/calibration-revision/plate-location-click-rim/quick-print/ink-well-type/z-axis/workflow-settings/camera-cal-store/pump suites green (1 pre-existing unrelated CV failure). |
| `MEBP_v75x_COMMON_PRINT_SETTINGS_PAGE.md` | v7.5.x new **Common Print Settings** page (Workflows mode, ⚙ tile) collecting the settings common to ALL workflows (operator request). **Two classes, one mutation bus:** (1) **Global params** — `pump_settle_time_s` (dwell), `pump_relief_volume_uL` (pressure relief), `pump_prime_time_s` (prime) — canonically on `HardwareConfig` (single source of truth the controller reads); one value, no override. (2) **Promoted prep defaults** — `service_z`/`prep_rate`/`oil_needles`/`buffer_needles`/`wash_cycles`/`wash_z_amp`/`wash_xy_amp`/`wash_dwell` — shared DEFAULTS each prep workflow **INHERITS** but can **OVERRIDE** locally (operator's chosen model). New pure-Python `SupportClasses/CommonPrintSettings.py` (proxies globals from a live HardwareConfig ref; holds promoted defaults dict persisted in settings.json `common_print_settings`; `get`/`set`-then-notify, `add_listener`, coercion). `WorkflowSettingsDialog` extended: `SettingsSection.add_common(key,label,widget,default,common_key=,overridable=)` — overridable adds an "Override" checkbox (off = disabled widget force-set to the common value, so the workflow's existing `widget.value()` runtime read returns the effective value unchanged; on = local value); non-overridable (globals) writes straight through via `common.set`. `dialog.set_common(model)` re-syncs links; `apply()` runs a **migration** (legacy saved value with no `__ovr` flag → override iff it differs from the common default, so existing customisations are preserved). New `gui/pages/workflows/common_print_settings_workflow.py` page (Pump-global + Needle-prep-defaults + read-only calibration heights) edits the model directly; tile in `workflow_picker.py`; dispatch + `set_common_print_settings` fan-out in `workflows_mode.py`. `gui/app.py` owns the model (restores promoted from settings.json, refreshes its HardwareConfig ref on every config change, listener persists globals→HW config / promoted→settings.json + re-fans-out, startup fan-out). The 4 prep workflows (spheroid/cell-targeting/cell-labeling/quick-print) promote their prep fields to `add_common` + add a "Common — Pump (global)" section + `set_common_print_settings`→`dlg.set_common`; fluorescence/stress/timing untouched (no prep). **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1** (edit common dwell/relief/prep default → reflected on Hardware Setup + inherited by un-overridden workflows; per-workflow Override holds a local value; restart preserves). Tests: `tests/test_v75x_common_print_settings.py` (22); workflow-settings/spheroid/cell-targeting/cell-labeling/quick-print/pump-settle/pump-plunger suites green (230). |
| `MEBP_v75x_PUMP_POST_ASPIRATE_PRESSURE_RELIEF.md` | v7.5.x **post-aspirate pump pressure relief — a setup parameter** (operator request). After picking up a reagent (ink/oil/buffer) the residual needle vacuum ingests air during inter-well travel (compliance); dispensing a small, experimentally-derived volume back at the pickup well bleeds it. Mirrors the sibling global pump params: new `HardwareConfig.pump_relief_volume_uL` (µL, default 0 = disabled, non-neg-coerced, serialized) + `StageController.pump_relief_volume_uL()` reader + a `relieve` kwarg on `StageController.move_pump_uL`. When `relieve=True` AND the move is an ASPIRATE (`volume_uL < 0`), after the aspirate+settle completes it dispenses `pump_relief_volume_uL` back via a single recursive `move_pump_uL` (positive → the `<0` guard makes it non-recursive); no-op when volume is 0 / it's a dispense / `relieve=False`. Relief fires **at the pickup well before any travel** (inside the same `move_pump_uL`). `PickAndPlaceManager._settled_pump_move(relieve=)` passthrough (TypeError fallback chain: full→settle-only→plain); `relieve=True` set at the reagent-pickup sites only — `run_prep` oil+buffer aspirates, `run_post_clean` buffer reload, `prepare_starting_oil` oil aspirate, `aspirate_ink` (Quick Print). **Deliberately NOT** applied to volume-balanced captures (spheroid/cell pull, cell-labeling, trypsin/dye) — they must keep exactly what they aspirated. **GUI:** Hardware Setup → Pump "Pump Timing (all pumps)" group gains a "Pressure relief" µL spinbox (wired into `_rebuild_config`/`_apply_config_to_ui`). Net loaded = aspirate − relief (intended; keep relief small). **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1** (set ~0.2–1 µL; confirm small dispense-back per pickup, no air ingestion on travel, print still flows; tune experimentally). Tests: `tests/test_v75x_pump_settle_and_prime_time.py` (31, +`TestPumpRelief`); spheroid/cell-targeting/cell-labeling/quick-print-pick-place (106, unaffected — relief defaults 0) + pump-plunger/workflow-settings offscreen GUI suites green. |
| `MEBP_v75x_PREP_BUFFER_PUMP_DRAIN_BEFORE_TRAVEL.md` | v7.5.x **fix — printing-prep "buffer" step times out and the run aborts before picking up ink** (operator: buffer pickup then "something times out, we never pick up the ink"; recurring). Log: `move_pump_uL(P2, -27.222 µL, 1.000 µL/s)` (4-needle buffer aspirate) → "Prep complete" → `flush_moves: M400 timed out after 15.0s (rx_lines=8, busy=8)` → `safe_travel_to: Z retract M400 timed out — ABORTING`. **Root cause:** buffer prep and the working ink pickup run the **identical** code (`PickAndPlaceManager._settled_pump_move` → `StageController.move_pump_uL(settle=True)`); the only difference is **volume**. A pump `G0` returns on Marlin `ok` = *admitted to the planner*, NOT motion-complete, and the settle-completion wait was `time.sleep(min(move_s, 10s))` — **capped at 10 s**. The 4-needle buffer aspirate (~27 µL @ 1 µL/s ≈ **27 s**) was truncated to 10 s, so `move_pump_uL` returned ~17 s early while Marlin kept running the pump. The next step (`aspirate_ink`→`safe_travel_to`) commanded a Z retract + `M400`; Marlin executes moves **sequentially**, so the retract queued *behind* the still-running pump move and `M400` timed out at 15 s (`busy=8` = board alive, just still draining). Ink pickup "worked" only because its ~6.5 µL fit under the 10 s cap. **Fix:** new `StageController._wait_pump_move_complete(move_s)` drains the in-flight pump move via `M400` (`ZPStage.flush_moves`, same mechanism `safe_travel_to` uses for Z) with a timeout **scaled to the estimate** (`move_s + 5 s`, backstop-capped at 180 s; poller suspended; exception-safe); `move_pump_uL(settle=True)` calls it instead of the 10 s-capped sleep, falling back to an open-loop sleep of the **full** estimate only when no `flush_moves` is available (older controller/fake). Constants: drop `_PUMP_MOVE_WAIT_CAP_S`; add `_PUMP_MOVE_DRAIN_TIMEOUT_CAP_S`/`_PUMP_MOVE_DRAIN_MARGIN_S`. Streamed print path + manual jog (`settle=False`) untouched; all discrete `settle=True` callers (prep/clean/cell/trypsin/dye/print prime) benefit. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1** (prep: buffer aspirate completes → travel-to-ink no M400 timeout → ink picked up → print starts; `logs/zp_serial.log` M400 after buffer returns `ok`). Tests: `tests/test_v75x_pump_settle_and_prime_time.py` (23, +`TestMovePumpSettleM400Confirm`); spheroid/cell-targeting/cell-labeling/quick-print-pick-place/simple-pm/ZP/stress suites green (pre-existing unrelated `test_v75x_print_setup_routine` prime-amount failures confirmed present with this fix stashed). |
| `MEBP_v75x_PRINTING_MODE_USES_CALIBRATED_WELLS.md` | v7.5.x **fix — Printing-mode print drove to the stage origin (0,0) at the wrong Z and ignored calibration** (operator: calibrated the plate + assigned inks/washes, hit Run, the needle went to `(0,0)` and never reached the assigned wells). Log proof: `PRINT: well C2 — first point (-22.5, -42.9)` → `XY clamped: (-22518.5,-42936.4) → (0.0,0.0)` → `wait_for_xy_arrival timeout`. **Root cause:** the ENTIRE Printing-mode print path computed every well centre **geometrically** — `WellPlate.get_well_position(name)` returns a PLATE-LOCAL A1-relative offset (A1 at origin) — × `controller.plate_axis_sign()` (`(-1,-1)` on ME3B V1), then fed it to `move_xy_absolute(from_zero_ref=True)` as a zero-ref mm coordinate. With `zero_position` = the needle-zero `(0,0)`, the well grid was anchored at the **stage origin** and the `(-1,-1)` flip made every print/travel well NEGATIVE → clamped to `(0,0)`; service wells (`_find_well`, **unsigned** raw geometric) went to `(0,19300)`/`(19300,0)` — also wrong. The **taught/calibrated** positions (C2 = `(86028,28274)` µm, A1 = `(107561,68633)` µm) live on the Calibration page (`_calibrated_positions`, exposed via `get_calibration_data()`), were wired to **Jog + Workflows** but **never to the Printing-mode setup page** (Quick Print was unaffected — it already resolves calibrated first). So the print ignored calibration entirely; the "wrong Z" was the needle descending at the clamped `(0,0)` corner. **Fix (one resolver, calibrated → zero-ref mm, geometric × sign fallback = byte-identical legacy):** new `PrintSettings.well_positions_mm` (calibrated zero-ref mm by name, stamped at job-build time) + new `PrintTrajectoryPlanner.resolve_well_xy_mm(name, plate, settings)`; `_well_xy` delegates to it (planner stores `self._settings` in `generate()`/`generate_inwell_print()`) → fixes the trajectory + all service wells. `HybridPlanExecutor` (the DEFAULT mode that ran) gains `_well_xy_mm` used in `_execute_print_step` / `TRAVEL_XY` / `_execute_service` (resolves the role-matched well via the resolver, not `_find_well`'s raw offset) / `estimate_time`. `print_setup_legacy` gains `set_calibration_data` + `_well_xy_zref_mm` (discrete `_build_job` resolves calibrated; `_get_settings` stamps `well_positions_mm`). `WizardPrintSetupPage.set_calibration_data` forwards to `_legacy`; `gui/app.py` wires `cal_page.calibration_data_changed → setup_page.set_calibration_data` + pushes once at startup (mirrors the Jog/Workflows wiring). `PrintPlanOfAction._signed_well_xy` also delegates to the resolver (latent fix; only the test-only `plan_to_commands` path uses it). Safety invariants (retract-before-XY, end-at-safe-Z, plate-bottom floor) untouched — only *where* XY travels is corrected. **SECOND bug, same cause — "Z was not moving up between wells":** the detached wizard page also can't reach app settings (`_app_settings` never assigned + `parent=None`), so `travel_z_height` stayed at the **5.0 mm** spinbox default and `print_z_height` at **0.1 mm**. On ME3B V1 (plate bottom 6.06, larger zero-ref Z = higher) **5.0 mm is BELOW the plate**, so "retract to safe Z" drove the needle **DOWN**. Fix sources these from already-wired data: `set_calibration_data`'s `safe_z` (= cal Fast-Move Z) drives `travel_z_spin` (range widened) → both paths retract to the real safe height; `_calibration_plate_bottom_z` prefers `controller.get_plate_bottom_z()` (pushed by `app.py::_update_print_floor_datum`) so `_apply_plate_relative_print_z` derives the right print Z; `_get_settings` sets `top_z_height` from `controller.get_plate_top_z()`. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1** (re-run the Box print → retracts to safe Z + travels to each taught well, service wells correct, prints at the right height, ends at safe Z). Tests: `tests/test_v75x_printing_mode_calibrated_wells.py` (16, incl. offscreen wizard-shell→legacy forward + travel/print-Z-from-calibration smoke); orientation/multi-object/planner-barrier/print-setup-routine/always-safe-z/z-retract/quick-print/trajectory/z-reference suites green. |
| `MEBP_v75x_PRINT_SETUP_PRINT_MANAGER_FORWARD.md` | v7.5.x **fix — Printing-mode Start was a silent no-op** ("`No print_manager on setup page`" logged once per Start press; print never launched). The v7.5.0 Print Builder refactor made the Printing-mode setup page the `WizardPrintSetupPage` shell, which composes the legacy `PrintSetupPage` as `self._legacy` — and the `PrintManager` is owned by the **legacy** page. `gui/app.py` reaches it as `setup_page.print_manager` from five sites (`_on_monitor_start` Start / `_wire_print_manager_to_monitor` recorder+bridge / Pause / Resume / Abort), but the wizard shell never forwarded the attribute, so `hasattr(setup_page,"print_manager")` was False → `_on_monitor_start` logged + returned and the others `hasattr`-guarded into no-ops. Fix = a read-only `print_manager` **property** on `WizardPrintSetupPage` → `getattr(self._legacy,"print_manager",None)` (returns the live manager so `setup.print_manager.recorder = …` still mutates the real object; fixes all five sites; **Quick Print** was unaffected — it owns its own manager). **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1** (Printing mode: build job → Monitor → Start launches; Pause/Resume/Abort work). Tests: `tests/test_v75x_print_setup_print_manager_forward.py` (4); quick-print/print-setup-routine/hybrid suites green (22). |

| `MEBP_v75x_PUMP_PLUNGER_CAL_AND_ASPIRATE_DISPENSE.md` | v7.5.x **per-pump plunger zero/max calibration (mirrors the Z stage) + ubiquitous ASPIRATE/DISPENSE vocabulary** (operator request). **Calibration:** new `StageController.apply_pump_setup(pump, raw_dispensed, raw_aspirated)` — the pump twin of `apply_z_setup`. ZERO = plunger all the way IN (syringe empty → fill 0); MAX = all the way OUT (full). One capture flow sets the datum (`zero_position[pump]`), **DERIVES** the dispense/aspirate direction, and sets the soft-limit envelope (`safety_limits.p{n}_min/max`, exact captured extremes — no margin, so the plunger is never commanded past its hard stops). Helpers: `pump_aspirate_sign`/`set_pump_aspirate_sign`, `is_pump_plunger_calibrated`, `pump_dir_sign` (= `−aspirate_sign` once calibrated, else legacy `_flip_sign`), `capture_current_pump_raw`, `apply_pump_convention` (startup restore), fill readout `pump_capacity_uL`/`raw_to_pump_fill_uL`/`pump_fill_uL`/`get_pump_setup`. **Direction OWNERSHIP (operator chose "just like Z"):** `move_pump_relative` applies `pump_dir_sign` (was `_flip_sign`); the Xbox jog (`ZPJogHandler`) gains a `pump_dir_sign_provider` and uses it for pump deltas — so a calibrated pump's direction comes from the calibration, retiring the per-pump `steps_per_mm`-sign/`axis_flip` hack. Uncalibrated pumps keep the legacy path (no change until calibrated). **Persistence:** `DeviceProfile.pump_setup` (×5 methods) + `main.py` `apply_pump_convention` restore; `SafetyLimits.update_from_hardware_config(skip_pumps=)` clobber-guard so the calibrated envelope wins over the syringe-stroke estimate. **GUI:** Hardware Setup → Pump "Pump Plunger Setup" group (pump selector + Set Dispensed/Set Aspirated + confirm dialog + capacity readout, mirrors stage_panel's Z setup); control_panel P1/P2/P3 readout reframed to **fill µL** (0=empty→capacity=full, bar 0→capacity), raw mm in the tooltip, falls back to raw mm when uncalibrated. **Vocabulary (aspirate & dispense ubiquitous; NOT "inject"):** canonical block atop `move_pump_uL` + CLAUDE.md principle #4. `CommandType.EXTRUDE`→`DISPENSE` (member renamed, **value kept `"extrude"`** so exec-log manifests stay comparable); `PickPlaceExecutor.post_expel_needles`→`post_dispense_needles` + workflow widget key/label; `StageController.extrude_uL`→`dispense_uL` (+ thin alias); `WellSetup.WasteBehavior/SortedCellBehavior` `eject_rate_uL_s`→`dispense_rate_uL_s` with a translating `from_dict` shim (old saved JSON still loads); stress-test `do_extrude`/`_chk_extrude`/`_extrude_burst`→`dispense`; scattered expel/eject/draw strings+comments → dispense/aspirate. Carve-outs left as-is: print-physics `extrusion` (`FlowPhysics`/`GeometryEngine`), Marlin `M302`/`M83`, `prime`, `retract`, cell-targeting `push`/`pull`, spheroid `pickup`/`release`. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1** (per pump: jog all-in→Set Dispensed reads fill 0; all-out→Set Aspirated reads ≈capacity + direction_ok; confirm aspirate raises fill / dispense lowers it; soft limits stop over-aspirate/over-dispense; restart restores). Tests: `tests/test_v75x_pump_plunger_setup.py` (19, incl. offscreen GUI smoke); pump/print/cell-targeting/spheroid/quick-print/stress/workflow-settings suites green. |
| `MEBP_v75x_PLATE_ORIENTATION_CONVENTION.md` | v7.5.x **ONE canonical well-plate orientation everywhere: well A1 displays TOP-LEFT; stage physical 0,0 is BOTTOM-RIGHT** (ME3B V1 Prior origin bottom-right, +X/+Y toward top-left). Fixes three operator symptoms — screens disagreed visually (the 180° flip was a constant duplicated in 2 views, absent elsewhere), motion drove to the MIRRORED well (geometric well→stage prediction assumed plate axes ALIGN with stage axes, but on ME3B they're anti-aligned), and there was no documented standard. **Single source of truth:** new per-machine `plate_flip_180` (default True) — `DeviceProfile.plate_flip_180` + `config/hardware/devices/ME3B V1.json` + `StageController.plate_flip_180()`/`plate_axis_sign()` (`(-1,-1)` when flipped) + `set_plate_flip_180`, restored at startup via `apply_z_convention(plate_flip_180=)` (mirrors the `z_up_sign` plumbing; `getattr`-guarded for `__new__` stubs); a Hardware Setup → Device → Z-Axis-Setup checkbox persists it via `_persist_active_profile`. **Display (drives the flip from the setting, keeps the reflect-about-envelope-centre involution so clicks round-trip):** `JogWorkspaceView` + `PrintTrajectoryMonitorView` `_FLIP_DISPLAY_180` constant → instance `_flip_180` + `set_plate_flip_180`, pushed from `jog_control`/`quick_print_workflow`; **`print_monitor.PlateOverviewWidget`** hybrid fix (wells plate-local but the live needle was fed zero-ref stage-frame → mirrored) negates the needle when flipped. **Geometry (the data model `WellPlate` is plate-local and UNCHANGED):** `get_all_positions_from_a1`/`get_a1_from_plate_center`/`get_all_positions_from_plate_center`/`get_well_area_bounds_*` gain an optional `plate_axis_sign=(1,1)` (default = byte-identical legacy; bounds re-normalise min/max under a negative sign); all `calibration.py` geometric prediction/seed/fallback sites + `jog_control` startup seed + `SimulatedCamera._draw_wells` pass `controller.plate_axis_sign()`. **Warp interplay:** the sign is applied ONLY to the prediction; `PlateWarpCalibrator` is untouched, so a fresh teach re-solves on sign-corrected predictions and taught wells reproduce measured exactly (no double-apply). **Persistence guard (safety):** `_save_calibration` stamps `plate_flip_180`; `_load_calibration` discards the stale taught XY calibration (taught_a1/corner/warp/markers) + forces a re-teach when the stamp ≠ current (else the warp's predicted-leg drives to garbage); `CalibrationSnapshotStore` fingerprint carries orientation. **Print-execution motion (needle-safety-critical):** every GEOMETRIC `get_well_position`-based well centre is sign-mapped — `PrintManager.HybridPlanExecutor._plate_axis_sign`, `print_setup_legacy` well_positions, `PrintTrajectoryPlanner._well_xy` (incl. service wells) + `PrintPlanOfAction._signed_well_xy` via new `PrintSettings.plate_axis_sign` (stamped in the GUI builders), Quick Print's geometric fallback; calibrated/taught well centres are left as-is. Malformed sign degrades to aligned `(1,1)` (never crashes a print). `WellSetup` Z-plane fit left untouched (self-consistent plate-local frame). **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1** (re-teach a full plate, "Go To" named wells + run a small multi-well print → correct physical wells, ends at safe Z; loading a pre-orientation cal prompts a re-teach). Tests: `tests/test_v75x_plate_orientation_convention.py` (17); plate-centering/freeform-warp/predict-well/last-known-cal/jog-nav/sim-camera/trajectory-view/z-axis/plate-design/rosette suites green (188). **Addendum — mosaic remap (existing cal was stale after the flip → navigated to the mirror well; a bogus warp had absorbed the 180° as a ~79 mm correction):** the mosaic is ground truth (captured at trusted absolute stage positions), so wells are RE-DERIVED from the mosaic's detected centres and RELABELLED for the current orientation, stored DIRECTLY (no warp). New `SupportClasses/MosaicWellRemap.py` (`label_positions` = orientation-aware relabel of fixed centres — A1 = max-stageX/maxY corner under `(-1,-1)`; `detect_raw_positions`/`detect_well_positions` = detect saved mosaic + back-project). New explicit `calibrated_positions` persistence in `calibration.py` (`_save_calibration` writes it when set with no warp; `_load_calibration` restores it DIRECTLY and SUPPRESSES warp/affine reconstruction, added to the orientation-guard pop-list; `_has_plate_calibration` recognises it). New Plate-Location button **"Re-derive wells from saved mosaic (ground truth)"** → `_ploc_rederive_from_saved_mosaic` (clears stale taught/warp/affine, relabels the most-complete ground-truth source, stores directly, NO motion). One-shot `tools_regen_plate_orientation.py` already regenerated the 24-well `settings.json` + `last_calibration.json` (new A1 = (104915,68044), dropped the bogus warp/affine; `*.bak-orientremap` backups). **Follow-ups:** (a) `_CalibrationPlateView` showed "top-left well = D6" because its predicted/calibrated dots were positioned relative to a desync-able `_taught_a1` (with a latent µm/mm mix) — now drawn **by NAME on each well's plate-local grid cell** (A1 always top-left, robust to `taught_a1` desync); the regen also resyncs `taught_a1 == calibrated A1`. (b) Whole-plate **mosaic scan now defaults to the FULL reachable XY envelope** (new `_ploc_whole_plate_scan_bounds`) so the entire XY space is mapped (was the well-centre extent); rosette single-well override unchanged; the tile-count confirm dialog surfaces the size. Tests: `tests/test_v75x_mosaic_orientation_remap.py` (14). |
