| `MEBP_v75x_PUMP_SETTLE_AND_PRIME_TIME.md` | v7.5.x **global pump settle time + prime time, configured on Hardware Setup → Pump** (operator request). Two new `HardwareConfig` fields (`pump_settle_time_s`=0.0, `pump_prime_time_s`=0.25; serialized, non-neg-coerced). **Settle** = a blocking dwell applied **before AND after** every *discrete* pump actuation so the fluid/pressure settles and the workflow doesn't advance to the next step until the pump finishes. Centralized at the single µL chokepoint `StageController.move_pump_uL(..., *, settle=False)`: when `settle=True` it sleeps the settle time pre-move, issues the move, **blocks** for the (open-loop) completion (`abs(vol)/rate+0.1`, capped 10 s — byte-identical to the legacy `EXTRUDE` wait it subsumes), then sleeps the settle time post-move; `settle=0` still waits for completion. New `StageController.pump_settle_time_s()` reader. **Discrete callers opt in** (`settle=True`): `PrintManager` `EXTRUDE` handler (subsumes its manual wait; `TypeError` fallback for fakes), the hybrid/service `move_pump` (brackets explicitly to keep its abort-aware wait), and `PickAndPlaceManager` via new module helper `_settled_pump_move(ctrl, …)` at **all 24** spheroid/cell-removal/prep/`aspirate_ink`/trypsin/dye sites (`TypeError` fallback). **Scope decisions (operator-confirmed):** discrete actuations only — **NOT** the streamed per-segment print path (`_execute_print_path` untouched — per-segment settle would dwell→over-extrusion blobs) and **NOT** manual jog (`move_pump_relative` — must stay continuous); one **global** value (not per-pump); single value applied **both** before+after. **Prime** = pre-flow lead-in duration printing workflows use (prime volume = flow × time). Quick Print's per-run "Pre-flow lead-in" Advanced knob (`_preflow`/`_preflow_s`) now **seeds its default from `pump_prime_time_s`** via new `_prime_default_s()` (so the hardware-page value ports in; per-run override preserved). **GUI:** "Pump Timing (all pumps)" group on the Hardware Setup → Pump sub-page (Settle/Prime spinboxes; wired into `_rebuild_config` + `_apply_config_to_ui`). **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_pump_settle_and_prime_time.py` (17); pick-place/cell-removal/quick-print/print-setup/print-manager/stress/ZP suites green (199). |
| `MEBP_v75x_CELL_TARGETING_REMOVAL.md` | v7.5.x new **Cell Targeting & Removal** workflow (enables the stubbed `cell_targeting` tile) — trypsinize-in-place-then-extract, built on the Spheroid Pick & Place scaffold (reuses LiveTargetPicker pick=removal/place=placement + WorkspaceTargetView + XZSideView + StandardJogContextPanel + the `PickPlaceExecutor` bridge + `run_prep` + safe-Z primitives + `_reagent_prep` service-well helpers; spheroid page untouched). Operator sequence: (1) standard needle prep (waste→oil→wash→buffer, once); (2) load a **cell-release reagent** (e.g. trypsin) assigned to a reagent well; (3) travel to the removal (x,y) + lower to a removal Z (default 0.1 mm off the plate bottom); (4) **slowly push** a small reagent column = `needle.cross_section_area_mm² × push_depth` (default 0.1 mm); (5) wait a user-defined dwell; (6) **quickly pull** `×multiplier` (default 2×) the pushed volume; (7) travel to the user-defined placement + dispense; (8) **clean+reset** (waste→wash→buffer, once after the loop). Steps 1/8 bracket the loop; 2–7 run per picked pair. **Backend (`PickAndPlaceManager`):** new `OperationType.CELL_TARGET_REMOVAL` + `CellRemovalConfig` (`compute_release_volume_uL`/`compute_extract_volume_uL`, slow `push_speed_uL_s`/fast `pull_speed_uL_s`, removal/place Z offsets) + executor `reagent_well_pos`/`reagent_dip_z_mm` (+`__reagent__` resolution) + `do_post_clean`/`post_expel_needles`; `_execute_cell_removal` (load→travel→slow push→dwell→fast pull→travel→gentle dispense, **pump volume-balanced** −push+push−pull+pull=0); `run_post_clean()` run once after the loop in `execute_queue`. Removal/place heights reuse the executor's `pick_z_mm`/`place_z_mm` (zero-ref mm via `print_height_to_zref`, polarity-safe on ZDIR=-1). **GUI (`cell_targeting_workflow.py`, NEW):** cell-removal config row (removal/place Z, push depth, pull ×, dwell, slow push / fast pull speeds, bore), reagent picker (mirrors Quick Print's printable-ink combo), prep + "clean after" rows, Start gate (balanced pairs + Safe Z + ZP connected + calibrated plate bottom + reagent assigned/calibrated + needle inner Ø + service wells when prep/clean on). Every cross-position move routes through `safe_travel_to`; empty-`well_name` targets → full safe-Z travel; mid-run ZP drop → `AbortException`; `finally` ends at safe Z. Wired via `workflow_picker.py` (tile enabled) + `workflows_mode.py`. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_cell_targeting_removal.py` (21); spheroid/quick-print/picker-scaling suites green (79). |
| `MEBP_v75x_QUICK_PRINT_PICK_AND_PLACE_PREP.md` | v7.5.x **Quick Print runs the full bioprint sequence (needle prep → pick up the print's ink → print)** instead of assuming the needle is pre-loaded — mirrors the Spheroid Pick & Place setup. Operator-confirmed scope: **one ink per run** (keep single-object/single-well; the "ink list" = the one ink the selected pump dispenses); **pickup volume computed from the print** (`flow×(path_len/print_speed) + flow×_PREFLOW_S`, ×safety, floored to the prime); **ink override = choose the pump's ink from the library** (combo of printable inks — `ink_type` ∉ {wash,waste,buffer,oil} — that have a reagent location, defaulting to the pump's assigned ink). **Reuse:** `PickPlaceExecutor.run_prep()` (waste→oil→wash→buffer) + safe-Z primitives (`safe_travel_to`/`ensure_retracted_to`/`move_pump_uL`/`print_height_to_zref`/`print_z_dir`) + `build_well_plate_job(path_segments=, return_home=False)` + discrete `PrintManager` (existing print path unchanged). **New** `gui/pages/workflows/_reagent_prep.py` (shared `SERVICE_ROLES`/`service_well_names`/`resolve_service_positions`/`needle_volume_uL` free functions — spheroid page left untouched) + `PickPlaceExecutor.aspirate_ink(well_pos, volume_uL, *, bore, z_mm, rate_uL_s)` (safe-Z travel to `__ink__` + aspirate, abort-aware, ZP-down-guarded). **`quick_print_workflow.py`:** ink-override combo + readout, prep row (prep checkbox **default ON** + Service Z + Wash cycles + Ink dip Z + Pickup safety ×), `_refresh_setup_status` gate, `_compute_pickup_volume_uL`; `_on_print` restructured → gate (XY+ZP connected, ink→calibrated source well, Safe Z + plate bottom, prep service wells) → up-front "confirm setup" dialog → preflight worker (try/finally `_retract_to_safe_z`: `run_prep` → `aspirate_ink` → preposition) → **HANDS-FREE** `_on_prepositioned` (the mid-run position-confirm dialog was REMOVED; the up-front confirm is the sole gate; still refuses on preamble abort/error, mid-run ZP drop, or unconfirmed positioning → auto-abort at safe Z) → `PrintManager`. Turning prep OFF + ink "(none)" restores the legacy plain-print path (no preamble/dialog). Abort routes to executor (preflight) or PrintManager (print); ink/service/cal gates live in `_on_print` (status text), not `_update_button_state` (preserves button-gating tests). **Post-print cleanup (operator follow-on):** after a print COMPLETES, a "Clean needle after print (waste → wash → reset oil)" row (checkbox **default ON** + Waste ×needle (6) + Oil refill ×needle fallback) runs `PickPlaceExecutor.run_print_cleanup()` on a worker thread — expel 6× needle to waste, wash, then refill OIL to reset the syringe's oil level by returning the plunger to its **pre-run position** (`move_pump_uL(bore, baseline − current)`, read live at the oil well; sign-safe via the shared `get_pump_position_uL`/`move_pump_uL` µL↔mm frame; ±20×-needle clamp + fixed-needle fallback). `_on_print` captures the pre-run pump position + gates on waste/wash/oil wells; `_on_state` triggers it on `PrintState.COMPLETED` only (abort/error skip it); reuses Service Z + Wash cycles. **Also (operator follow-on): a `Print speed: N% of max` spin (1–100%, default 25%)** — a single master lever that scales BOTH the XY traverse (`print_speed_mm_s = pct × XY max`) and the pump flow (`flow = pct × Flow@100%`; the Flow knob is relabelled **Flow @100%**) for every print-path segment, so the deposited bead width (volume-per-mm) is invariant to the % (only overall speed changes — `PrintManager._execute_print_path` paces by `max(xy_move_time, pump_move_s)`, both now scale together). 100% anchor = measured XY top speed (`PrintTimingCalibrationStore.get_store().get_xy_max_speed_um_s()`) else `safety_limits.max_xy_speed` (µm/s→mm/s); `_xy_max_mm_s`/`_resolved_print_kinematics` feed both `_build_settings` and `_compute_pickup_volume_uL`; resolved `mm/s · µL/s` shown in the setup status. Scoped to Quick Print. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_quick_print_pick_and_place.py`. |
| `MEBP_v75x_NEEDLE_USE_LAST_KNOWN_LOCATION.md` | v7.5.x **"✓ Use last known location" button on Calibration → Needle Location** so an operator sure nothing changed can skip the camera-based Center & Save re-centering. | `MEBP_v75x_PICK_PLACE_PREP_ROUTINE.md` | v7.5.x **pick & place needle-conditioning PREP routine** (operator-specified): once before the pick/place loop — (1) safe-travel→waste, expel 1 needle of oil; (2) →oil, draw 1 needle of oil; (3) →wash, wash; (4) →buffer, draw 4 needles of buffer. **Decisions (operator-confirmed):** service-well locations **inherited from Hardware Setup → Ink → Reagent Locations** (a well's role = the assigned ink's `ink_type` string `oil/wash/waste/buffer`; oil has no `WellRole` so match the string, not `well_role_for_ink_type`); **"1 needle"** = inner-bore cylinder `π·(id/2)²·length` µL (new `NeedleSpec.internal_volume_uL` = `cross_section_area_mm2×length_mm`); **wash** = dip + Z jiggle (lift-first via `move_z_user_relative(+amp)`, return to the EXACT dip Z via `move_z_absolute` so a clamped lift can't walk the tip toward the glass) + random XY jiggle about the well centre + recentre. **Backend (`PickAndPlaceManager`):** executor gains `oil_well_pos`+`__oil__`, prep config (`do_prep`, `prep_bore`, `needle_volume_uL`, `oil_needles`=1, `buffer_needles`=4, `service_z_mm`, wash params); `run_prep()` built from `_safe_move_to_well(key, target_z_mm=service_z)` (full safe-Z travel, ZP-disconnect-guarded, abort-aware) + `move_pump_uL(bore, ±n·unit)` (+expel/−draw, matching the spheroid handler); `_do_wash()`; `execute_queue` runs prep once inside the try (the `finally: _retract_to_safe_z()` still guarantees end-at-safe-Z). **GUI (`spheroid_pickup_workflow`):** prep row (checkbox default-on + Service Z + Wash cycles + live status), `_needle_volume_uL`/`_service_well_names` (ink_locations×ink_type→role)/`_resolve_service_positions` (role→absolute µm via calibrated `_well_positions`)/`_refresh_prep_status`; `_on_start` resolves + **gates** on needle geometry + all 4 service wells (assigned+calibrated) + plate-bottom Z, then wires the executor; also fixed the needle-viz `outer_diameter_mm`→`od_mm` latent bug. Verified adversarially (2 lenses, both pass; 2 LOW latent items hardened). **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_spheroid_pick_place_z.py` (`TestNeedleVolume`+`TestPrepRoutine`, 16 in file: order, volumes, wash lift-first+recentre, prep-off, missing-well abort+retract, ZP-disconnect refuse) + offscreen page-resolution smoke; spheroid/jog/z-retract suites green. |
| `MEBP_v75x_SPHEROID_PICK_PLACE_Z_AND_PUMP.md` | v7.5.x **fix Spheroid Pick & Place EXECUTION** (follow-on to `MEBP_v75x_SPHEROID_PICK_CLICK_SCALING.md`; the click→stage scaling was already fixed). Three operator reports: (1) crash `move_pump_uL() got an unexpected keyword argument 'feedrate_mm_min'`; (2) "Z never went to the location" + ~30 s to move; (3) need separate, configurable pick/place Z offsets from the plate bottom. **Causes:** **(crash)** `StageController.move_pump_uL(pump, volume_uL, rate_uL_s=None)` takes **µL/s** and converts+flow-clamps internally, but `PickAndPlaceManager` pre-converted via `_uL_s_to_mm_min` and passed `feedrate_mm_min=` (not a kwarg) → TypeError; present at every `move_pump_uL` call (spheroid×2, trypsin×4, fluorescent×5). **(Z wrong+slow)** the spheroid page created the `PickPlaceExecutor` but never set `safe_z_mm`/operating Z → defaults `safe_z=5.0`/`operating_z=0.0`; and the clicked targets carry `well_name=""`, so `_safe_move_to`'s `well_name != _current_well` (`""!=""` False) treated the move as **intra-well** → a relative ±`intra_well_retract_mm` (1 mm) Z jiggle around 0.0 instead of travelling to the operate height; the two `wait_for_z_arrival(target=-1.0 then 0.0)` could never reach the real ~40 mm Z → two 15 s timeouts ≈ the "slow move". **Fix:** **(1)** all `move_pump_uL` → `rate_uL_s=<speed_uL_s>` (removes redundant `_uL_s_to_mm_min`, keeps the flow-rate safety clamp). **(2)** `SpheroidPickupConfig` gains `pick_z_offset_mm`(0.10)/`place_z_offset_mm`(0.50) = heights above plate bottom (mm, independent); executor gains `pick_z_mm`/`place_z_mm` (zero-ref mm; None→`operating_z_mm`); `_safe_move_to(target, target_z_mm)` + `_intra_well_move(target_z_mm)` lower to that Z, and the intra-well shortcut fires **only for a non-empty well name matching the previous move** (empty well name = arbitrary clicked points → always full safe-Z travel); `_execute_spheroid_pickup` uses pick_z at source, place_z at dest. **(3)** spheroid page adds **Pick Z (↑ bottom)** + **Place Z (↑ bottom)** spins; `_plate_offset_to_zref` resolves via `controller.print_height_to_zref` (fallback: page `plate_bottom_z` × `print_z_dir`); `_on_start` resolves pick/place Z, **gates on Safe Z + a calibrated plate bottom** (clear status message else), and sets `executor.safe_z_mm/pick_z_mm/place_z_mm`. **Retract-before-XY hole (4-agent audit, HIGH):** normal (ZP-connected) path is safe — both pick & place route `_safe_move_to`→`safe_travel_to` (raise Z→M400+`wait_for_z_arrival`→abort-if-unconfirmed→XY), and the 1 mm intra-well shortcut is unreachable for empty-`well_name` spheroid targets. BUT `safe_travel_to` wraps its retract+confirm in `if is_zp_connected:` and the XY move in a *separate* `if is_xy_connected:`, so a **ZP USB drop (CH340) while XY stays connected** skips the retract and still drives XY — dragging a needle left **down** (the spheroid op has no final retract → ends at `place_z`). Fix (pick&place path): `_safe_move_to` raises `AbortException` when `is_zp_connected` is False (mid-run drop), `_on_start` gates on `is_zp_connected` (start-time). **Root fix (Addendum 2, both follow-ups done):** a 3-agent recon confirmed there is NO XY-only/no-needle production caller and `is_zp_connected` short-circuits True in sim, so a real-HW False = "needle present, board dropped". **(a)** `StageController.safe_travel_to` hardened — new `_needle_present()` (connected now / `_zp_ever_connected` sticky session flag set on first real ZP connect / `_hardware_config.pumps` configured) drives an `elif self._needle_present(): return False` on the retract block, so a ZP-disconnect with a needle present **refuses** the XY move (poller still resumed) instead of dragging it; a genuine no-needle rig still moves XY. Fixes the root for ALL callers (jog/calibration/quick-print/stress/spheroid). **(b)** `PickPlaceExecutor.execute_queue` wraps its loop in try/finally → new `_retract_to_safe_z()`→`ensure_retracted_to(safe_z_mm)` (raise-only, no-op when retracted/ZP-down, never raises) on every exit (completion/abort/error), so a finished/aborted pick&place no longer leaves the needle parked at `place_z` (mirrors `MEBP_v75x_ALL_PRINTS_END_AT_SAFE_Z.md`). **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_spheroid_pick_place_z.py` (11) + `test_v731_jog_navigation` (`test_refuses_xy_when_needle_present_but_zp_down`, `…_pumps_configured…`, `test_xy_only` reframed as true no-needle); spheroid-picker-scaling + all `safe_travel_to`-consumer suites green; offscreen page-build smoke OK. |
| `MEBP_v75x_XY_ZP_TIMING_CALIBRATION.md` | v7.5.x **Workflows → XY↔ZP Timing Calibration** tile (⏱️) — measures the controller's per-segment **phase lag** so the move↔ZP timing can be allocated to keep the pump synced to the needle (the XY/Prior is streamed open-loop and lags the commands, so the pump finishes a path before the needle does → dry tail). **Method (current, after the optical-flow approach was scrapped as "a very big fail"):** issue a straight LINE of N collinear segment moves back-to-back (paced like a print), watch the microscope frames with a plain **frame-difference** test (`_FrameMotion`: mean abs diff of downscaled+blurred grayscale — "are the frames the same?", NO optical flow / no direction) until they STOP changing, and log the **delay from the last command to stillness**. Sweep N=1,2,3…: the delay grows with N as the open-loop stream outruns the stage and a backlog accumulates; a least-squares fit (`_fit_line`) gives **slope = per-segment phase lag**, intercept = base settle. A single jog-speed scale can't fix this — the slope is the per-segment timing to allocate. Needle retracted at Safe Z throughout; microscope required; poller+watchdog suspended; baseline still-noise → threshold. **Store** `SupportClasses/PrintTimingCalibrationStore.py` `by_phase` section (`update_phase`/`get_phase`, run-weighted, atomic `config/hardware/print_timing_calibration.json`) + per-N JSONL to `logs/timing/`. **Live view:** `CameraFeedView` + `_MotionStrip` (frame-change vs time with command/still markers — watch the frames go flat) + `_DelayPlot` (delay-vs-N with the fitted phase-lag line). Wired via `workflow_picker.py` + `workflows_mode.py`. The **measurement is the deliverable** (understand the timing first); the implied fix is ADDITIVE per-segment pacing (add `slope`/segment) — NOT yet wired (the earlier multiplicative `correction_factor` auto-pacing was reverted from `PrintManager._execute_print_path` per operator feedback that jog-speed scaling isn't the fix). History: the scrapped optical-flow approach (phase-correlation flow direction, `TurnLagAnalyzer`, measured-vs-commanded divergence plot, verify rerun, auto-apply) is described in the plan's Rev 2–6; `TurnLagAnalyzer.py` deleted. **Rev 9 — ROOT CAUSE the first real sweep exposed (XY ~2.8× too slow; delay grew ~875 ms/segment monotonically → backlog → software, per the operator):** a 5-agent workflow (`xy-timing-rootcause`) found (1) `XYStage._protocol_max_speed_um_s` was set ONLY in the sim branch, so real-HW `set_speed_mm_s(1.0)` fell back to a hardcoded 50000 µm/s assumption → `SMS,2` (2% of the TRUE top speed) → the loop paces 500 ms but the move takes ~1375 ms → Prior backlogs streamed `G` commands; (2) acceleration is NEVER set → short moves accel-dominated. Fixes: `XYStage._max_speed_um_s()` resolver (measured override→sim→protocol `max_speed`→50000-with-warning) + `set_max_speed_um_s()` setter, used by `set_speed_mm_s`/`set_velocity` (round not int-trunc); `set_acceleration(80)` before print/timing moves; dup hardcoded-50000 fallback removed. CAVEAT: proscan_iii.json `max_speed` is still an unverified 50000 — the conversion is correct PLUMBING but the value needs the REAL stage top speed (`set_max_speed_um_s` / a measurement); so the ACCEL fix may help immediately, the SPEED only once the true max is set. Tests: `tests/test_v75x_xy_speed_conversion.py` (8). **Rev 10 — top-speed measurement (pins the true max) + selectable start:** new **"Measure top speed"** button → `_run_top_speed` at FULL speed (`SMS,100`) sweeps single-move DISTANCES along +X, times each to stillness (reuses `_measure_n` N=1 + frame-diff + `_calibrate_threshold`), fits time-vs-distance → **slope = 1/top-speed** (accel/overhead cancels into the intercept); STORES (`set/get_xy_max_speed_um_s`) + applies live (`XYStage.set_max_speed_um_s`), and `StageController.connect_stages` re-applies on every real XY connect (prints/jog correct across restarts). Selectable **start location** ("Use current position" captures live XY / "Plate centre"; `_resolve_start_mm`). `_DelayPlot` parametrised (headline/x/y labels) for both modes; `logs/timing/topspeed_*.jsonl`. Flow: Measure top speed → re-run settle sweep (lag should drop). **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_print_timing_calibration.py` (incl. start-capture + top-speed measure→store→apply); full `test_v75x*` = 882 (1 pre-existing unrelated CV failure). |
| `MEBP_v75x_SPHEROID_PICK_CLICK_SCALING.md` | v7.5.x **fix Spheroid Pick & Place live-view click→stage conversion** (two operator reports: "wrong resolution → picked spot doesn't match" and "scale of motion way off / orders of magnitude", seen on HW as `wait_for_xy_arrival timeout target=(64735.02, 49557.15) actual=(114.332, 76.645)` = stage drove to the XY-envelope corner). **Bug A (1000×, catastrophic):** `StageController.get_xy_position(cached=True)` returns **absolute stage µm** (`get_xy_position_mm` divides by 1000; the Jog page does `xy[0]-zero["x"]` no scaling), but the spheroid workflow treated it as **mm** and ×1000 → `LiveTargetPicker._read_stage_xy_mm`/`_pixel_to_stage_um` (`sx_um=stage*1000`) inflated every picked target ~1000× (stage ~64735 µm → target ~64.7 M µm → executor `safe_travel_to`/`move_xy_absolute_um` clamps to the envelope corner, `wait_for_xy_arrival((target-zero)/1000≈64735 mm)` times out = the logged numbers); same ×1000 in `SpheroidPickupWorkflowPage._refresh_position_indicators` + picker `_refresh_stage_position`. **Bug B (resolution scale):** objective µm/px measured at a recorded resolution (`objectives.json` e.g. `916×686`) but applied to the live frame regardless of size — µm/px ∝ 1/width, so 2×/4× live res = 2×/4× error (the objective card already *recorded* the cal resolution + warns ⚠; nothing *used* it live). Fix: **(1)** `CameraManager` resolution-aware — `_um_per_px_res[]` + `set_um_per_px(…, resolution=None)` + `effective_um_per_px(cam_idx, live_w)=value×calib_w/live_w` + `get_um_per_px_resolution`; `pixel_to_stage_offset` resolves µm/px against the LIVE `image_w` (getattr-guarded so fakes exposing only `get_um_per_px` still work — also fixes Plate-Location click-rim). **(2)** `objective_calibration_card` passes `resolution=` on both `set_um_per_px` pushes. **(3)** `live_target_picker` `_read_stage_xy_mm`→`_read_stage_xy_um` (no ×1000); `_refresh_um_per_px` resolves `(base, cal_res)` + pushes to mgr; new `_sync_live_um_per_px` rescales to the live frame width + pushes to overlay view + readout (`cal X@W → live Wpx`). **(4)** spheroid `_refresh_position_indicators` `xy[0]-zero["x"]` (no ×1000), matching Jog. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_spheroid_picker_scaling.py` (12); plate-location/z-retract/objective/camera/quick-print suites green (23/24 `test_real_24_well_mosaic` is the documented pre-existing CV failure). |
| `MEBP_v75x_PRINT_PATH_XY_END_DRAIN.md` | v7.5.x **fix XY/ZP de-sync at the END of a print** (surfaced once the ZP feedrate root-cause fix stopped the board drops): "the Z retracts / the print reports done while the XY stage is still moving — a backlog of queued moves keeps it going." Cause: `PrintManager._execute_print_path` streams the path **open-loop** — each `move_xy_absolute` → Prior `G x,y` returns its `R` (received) ack immediately and moves **async**, paced only by `time.sleep` (which under-counts accel/decel ramps), so the Prior accumulates a **backlog**; the loop settles XY only at the **start**, never the **end**, so when the segment loop finishes `_execute_command` returns and the plan advances to `TRAVEL_UP` (Z retract) while the Prior is still draining queued moves. Fix: at the end of `_execute_print_path`, **drain the XY queue** — `_wait_for_xy_settle(final_x, final_y, timeout=30.0)` blocks until the stage physically reaches the final path point before returning (so Z retract / the next object's hop start in sync). Deliberately **no mid-path barrier** (stopping every N segments would dwell → over-extrusion blobs); drain only at the path boundary. Direct `cached=False` Prior query (works with the ZP poller suspended), honors abort, logs `settle_wait`. Also tightens multi-object prints (each object settles before its inter-object hop). Residual in-path open-loop lag is cosmetic (XY+pump lag together) — accel-aware pacing deferred. Tests: `tests/test_v75x_print_path_planner_barrier.py::TestPrintPathDrainsXYAtEnd` (2; file 13); print/quick-print/logging/trajectory/stress = 120 green. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1.** |
| `MEBP_v75x_SIMPLE_PRINT_MANAGER.md` | v7.5.x **`SimplePrintManager` — a deliberately MINIMAL, every-move-CONFIRMED print executor as a debug baseline** (bottom rung of an "add complexity back until it breaks" process for the ZP-drop hunt). New `SupportClasses/SimplePrintManager.py` consumes the SAME `build_well_plate_job` command plan but runs it as a straight blocking sequence — `retract→confirm → XY→confirm → descend→confirm → [prime] → per-segment(extrude+XY, then M400+XY-settle) → retract→confirm`. Every segment is confirmed (M400 drains the ZP move, XY-arrival poll drains the Prior move), so the Marlin planner buffer **never holds >1 move/board → can't saturate** — NO streaming/barriers/pacing/poller-suspend. **Keeps all safety:** retract-before-XY (confirmed), explicit feedrate on every move, descent confirmed (M400+M114) + **abort if unconfirmed**, abort on mid-print ZP drop, plate-bottom floor armed, ALWAYS end at safe Z. **Omits** (re-add incrementally): open-loop streaming, barriers, drift pacing, poller/watchdog suspend, hybrid/trajectory/velocity/service executors, resume/recorder/JSONL log. Drop-in interface (`load_job`/`start`/`abort`/`state`/`on_progress`). Wired into the **Stress Test** via a **"Simple PrintManager (debug)"** checkbox (`_StressConfig.use_simple_pm`) so the operator A/B-tests it vs the full `PrintManager` on the same HW (logs `[SimplePrintManager]`/`[PrintManager]`). If Simple prints reliably but Full doesn't, the streaming path is implicated; add-back ladder in the plan. Tests: `tests/test_v75x_simple_print_manager.py` (10); stress+simple+feedrate = 43 green. |
| `MEBP_v75x_ZP_Z_FEEDRATE_INHERITANCE_FIX.md` | v7.5.x **ROOT CAUSE of the entire ZP-disconnect saga — Z moves inherited the pump's slow modal feedrate (pure software, as the user insisted; NOT USB/thermal/hardware).** Decisive trace (`logs/zp_serial.log` 15:04): a print's retract emitted **`G0 Z39.5900` with NO feedrate**, the descent's `M400` then sat `busy` 12 s and timed out (`rx=6 busy=6 no-ok`), M114 went `busy`, then the board fell off USB (`ERROR_BAD_COMMAND`). The pump (P2 → Marlin **Y** on ME3B V1) streams `G0 F1.7999… Y` = **F1.8 mm/min**; Z and the pump are the **same Marlin board sharing modal `F`**, so a Z move with no explicit `F` inherited F1.8 → a ~21 mm retract became a **~12-MINUTE crawl** → Marlin stuck `busy` (M400 timeout) → Z motor creeps/overheats ("only Z gets hot") → CH340 ultimately drops off USB. **Only the print trips it** because only the print interleaves slow pump moves right before Z moves (jog/`safe_travel_to` always set their own `F`) — exactly the "flawless in jog/travel, fails immediately in print" report. Bug site: `ZPStageManager.move_absolute` emitted `F` only `if feedrate_mm_min` (omitted when None); the many `move_z_absolute(...)` callers (TRAVEL_UP, end-retract, hybrid/trajectory, VelocityExecutor) pass none (`move_relative` was already safe). Fix at two chokepoints: **(1)** `move_absolute` ALWAYS emits explicit `F` (falls back to `self.feedrate`=200, never stale modal); **(2)** `StageController.move_z_absolute` resolves a Z feedrate (`_zp_retract_feedrate`→`max_z_feedrate`→200) when None. Plus: `TRAVEL_UP`/`TRAVEL_DOWN` pass explicit retract/insert feedrates; `MOVE_Z` **aborts the print** (raise→ERROR+safe-Z retract) if Z can't be confirmed (the failed run dry-dragged with Z frozen at 39.59); stress `_print_burst` **stops the whole test** on a print ERROR/start-error/drop. Supersedes the thermal/USB framing of `MEBP_v75x_ZP_AUTO_RECONNECT_AND_FAST_Z.md` (Z heat + drop were downstream of the crawl). **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1** (confirm `logs/zp_serial.log` has NO bare `G0 Z…` and no multi-minute `busy` M400). Tests: `tests/test_v75x_zp_feedrate_inheritance_fix.py` (12); ZP/print/stress = 85 green; full `test_v75x*` = 787/788 (1 pre-existing unrelated CV failure). |
| `MEBP_v75x_ZP_AUTO_RECONNECT_AND_FAST_Z.md` | v7.5.x **sustained-load hardening after the planner-barrier fix** (a single print now runs clean, but the back-to-back stress loop still trips the USB-layer drop, with Z moves slowing first — "only Z gets hot"). **(1) Faster Z:** new `StageController._refresh_zp_move_feedrates()` derives Z feedrates from the configured Z max (retract=Z max; insert=0.6×) so a ~21 mm Z move finishes in ~2.5 s not ~6 s (less motor on-time/heat, within the M400 timeout); called from `apply_device_settings`; conservative 200/100 fallback. Print `MOVE_Z` descent now passes `feedrate_mm_min=_zp_insert_feedrate` (deterministic, not inherited). **(2) Auto-reconnect:** an unexpected `_handle_disconnect("ZP")` spawns `_zp_reconnect_loop` (`_schedule_zp_reconnect`) retrying `connect_zp(simulate=False)` 6× every 2 s — the CH340 re-enumerates on the same COM port, so the link self-heals without a restart; on success `on_connect("ZP")` → the GUI position-restore prompt (reopen DTR-resets Marlin). Gated real-HW + `auto_reconnect_zp`(default True) + not `_shutting_down` (set in `shutdown()`); getattr-safe; idempotent (`_zp_reconnecting`). A print in progress aborts (can't resume — position lost). Faster-Z is a mitigation for Z-driver heat (firmware levers: M84 idle timeout, M906 current); the USB drop is still USB-layer (disable Windows USB selective suspend). **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_zp_auto_reconnect_and_fast_z.py` (10); full `test_v75x*` = 775/776 (1 pre-existing unrelated CV failure). |
| `MEBP_v75x_PRINT_PATH_PLANNER_BARRIER.md` | v7.5.x **print-only ZP/USB drop fixed by bounding the Marlin planner buffer** (raw motions ran flawlessly but a real PRINT failed almost immediately: ZP `WriteFile ERROR_BAD_COMMAND`, "crawl" = pump cmds 4–10 s apart, `MOVE_Z not confirmed`). Root cause (multi-agent diff of `_execute_print_path` vs the passing stress components): the print is the ONLY path that issues a long **open-loop** stream — one pump G0 + one XY G0 per waypoint, paced only by XY transit time, **no M400 barrier between segments**. Marlin `ok`=admitted-to-planner-buffer (not move-done), so a long finely-sampled path admits faster than the board executes → ~16-block buffer fills → flow-control stall → CH340 write faults. Raw motions never fail because each CONFIRMS (M400+wait → drains buffer) or is net-zero/short. Incremental hardening (user-chosen over the full continuous-extrusion rethink): **(1)** M400 barrier every `_PATH_BARRIER_EVERY`(8) segments in `_execute_print_path` (bounds buffer depth; near-instant when shallow); **(2)** pace by the rate-limiting axis `_sleep_s = max(xy_move_time, seg_vol_uL/flow_rate_uL_s, 0.05)`; **(3)** suspend the port-health watchdog (new `ConnectionWatchdog.pause/resume` + `StageController.suspend/resume_zp_watchdog`) for PRINT_PATH alongside the poller, so only the print thread touches the ZP COM handle during the burst. Separate idle USB drop ⇒ disable Windows USB selective suspend (OS). Next-level rethink (deferred): continuous extrusion per object + path re-segmentation + spiral over-sampling fix. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_print_path_planner_barrier.py` (8); affected suites = 109 green. |
| `MEBP_v75x_ZP_SERIAL_TRACE_AND_COMPONENT_ISOLATION.md` | v7.5.x **robust ZP logging + stress-print component isolation** (diagnostics for the intermittent ZP fault). **(1) New `SupportClasses/ZPSerialTrace.py`** — `ZPSerialTracer` (lazy `tracer()`) → dedicated rotating `logs/zp_serial.log` (`propagate=False`, off the main log): per-command transactions (command, ack latency ms, outcome ok/timeout/error/reset, rx-line + busy counts), in-memory ring of last 300 txns **dumped on any anomaly/event** so the lead-up is captured; verbosity env `MEBP_ZP_TRACE` = 0/1(default summary)/full(byte-level TX+RX). `ZPStage._txn`/`_read_until_ok`/`flush_moves` feed it (`_read_until_ok`→`(ok,text,stats)`; sole caller `_txn`). **`flush_moves` now records `rx_lines` on timeout + a `SILENT`(rx=0=board truly quiet) vs `no-ok`(rx>0=ok stolen/move running) flag** — the key M400 diagnostic. **(2) `main.py`** adds a `RotatingFileHandler`→`logs/app.log` (always DEBUG) so the session log persists (plus the earlier `faulthandler`→`logs/crash.log`). **(3) `PrintExecutionLogger`** `sample` events now carry ZP comm health (`zp_cmd`/`zp_ok`/`zp_ok_fail`/`zp_reset`/`zp_conn`) so you see WHEN acks fail vs the motion. **(4) Stress test component isolation** — new individually-selectable print sub-ops to find the culprit: **Z descent+confirm** (`_descend_burst`+`_z_move_confirm`, reproduces the print MOVE_Z = drive Z down by a configurable safe depth + M400 + wait + retract; the prime suspect, the one move travel/jog never do) and **Pump extrude** (`_extrude_burst`, net-zero pump stream); config `do_descend`/`descend_depth_mm`/`do_extrude`/`component_reps`. Tests: `test_v75x_stress_test_workflow.py` (19); broad ZP/print/stress = 110 green. |
| `MEBP_v75x_ZP_M400_ATOMIC_FLUSH.md` | v7.5.x **root-cause fix for bogus "M400 timed out → ZP disconnected" on a live board** (software, not hardware). Decisive clue: a **zero-distance** end-of-print retract's M400 still timed out 15 s — a no-move M400 returns `ok` in ms, so the board answered and **our read missed the `ok`**. Cause: `flush_moves` (M400) was the one ZP transaction that **wasn't atomic** — it released `_serial_lock` between each `readline()`, so a **straggler concurrent ZP reader** (the poller does one more M114 after `suspend()` just sets a flag; the Xbox **jog-handler thread** is never suspended) slipped in and **consumed the M400 `ok`**. flush_moves then waited out the full timeout for an `ok` already eaten → callers escalate to "disconnected"/abort. The synchronous-`ok` change lengthened concurrent M114 holds, widening the steal window (rare→always). Fix: `flush_moves` now holds `_serial_lock` for the **entire** drain→write-M400→read-until-`ok` (atomic, like the command handshake) so the `ok` can't be stolen; `stop()`'s close-serialization acquire timeout raised 8→17 s to cover flush_moves' max hold (preserves `MEBP_v75x_ZP_CLOSE_DURING_READ_CRASH.md`). **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1** (run with the Xbox connected — the likely straggler). Tests: `test_flush_moves_holds_lock_atomically` in `tests/test_v75x_zp_serial_flow_control.py`; ZP+print = 112 green. |
| `MEBP_v75x_ZP_CLOSE_DURING_READ_CRASH.md` | v7.5.x **fix hard Python crash "always on the Z-retract-to-safe at the end of prints"** (no traceback). Root cause = **concurrent `serial.close()` during a blocked `readline()`**: when the ZP board goes silent during the end-of-print retract, the retract thread is blocked in `serial.readline()` (flush_moves' 15 s M400 wait / the new synchronous `ok` handshake) **holding `_serial_lock`**, while the watchdog/poller thread runs `_handle_disconnect→disconnect_zp→zp_stage.stop()→serial.close()` which took **no lock** — closing a USB-serial (CH340) handle mid-read is a hard crash on Windows. The synchronous flow-control change widened the window (~30 s retract on a dead board vs ~15 s) so it went rare→always. Fix: `ZPStageManager.stop()` + `XYStageManager.stop()` now acquire `_serial_lock` (bounded: ZP=`DEFAULT_OK_TIMEOUT_S+2`, which exceeds `_read_until_ok`'s continuous lock-hold; XY=5 s; **getattr-safe** for `__new__` stand-ins) **before** `close()`, so the close waits for the in-flight read to finish (then the next read hits a closed port + fails gracefully). Plus `main.py` enables `faulthandler` (all threads → `logs/crash.log` + stderr) so future hard crashes leave a traceback. Follow-up DONE (adaptive fail-fast): `send_data` `ok`-wait drops to `SILENT_OK_TIMEOUT_S`=0.5 s (from 6 s) once the board is known silent (`_last_position_read_ok` False; a successful ack restores it), and `flush_moves` M400 caps to ~2 s when silent — so a dead-board retract no longer hangs ~30 s and a marginal board can't cascade into multi-second hangs that also starve the shared-poller XY reads. See `MEBP_v75x_ZP_SERIAL_FLOW_CONTROL.md` addendum. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_zp_close_during_read_crash.py` (4); ZP/XY/print/stress = 93 green. |
| `MEBP_v75x_STRESS_TEST_WORKFLOW.md` | v7.5.x new **ZP Stress Test** Workflows tile (`stress_test`, enabled) — a one-button repeatable harness to **bench-validate the synchronous-`ok` ZP flow-control fix** on real ME3B V1. Loops, over N cycles on a worker thread (GUI/camera stay live): **Travel stress** (`safe_travel_to` hops, `target_z_mm=None` = retract→XY, never lowers — the exact failed path), **Jog stress** (dense small Z + optional net-zero pump relative jogs, `ensure_retracted_to(safe_z)` first — the buffer-flood scenario), **XY stress** (CONCURRENT XY/Prior oscillation on its own thread *during the jog phase* — drives the XY serial channel at the same time as the ZP channel to surface cross-communication conflicts; needle retracted so plate motion is safe; excluded from print/travel phases which own XY), **Print stress** (opt-in, default off + flow 0 dry — small synthesized circle via real discrete `PrintManager`). Monitors `ZPStageManager.get_comm_counters()` (new telemetry: `cmd`/`ok`/`ok_fail`/`reset`) + per-loop `is_zp_connected` AND `is_xy_connected` transitions + `_board_reset_detected`; live counter grid + event log + **PASS/FAIL banner (PASS iff 0 comms drops on either channel, 0 resets, 0 unacked)**. Safety: refuses to start without XY+ZP connected AND Safe Z set (retract to 0 = crash on ZDIR=-1); all travel retract-first; XY oscillation is net-zero + envelope-clamped + only while the needle is retracted; `finally` always retracts to Safe Z; cooperative Stop. New `gui/pages/workflows/stress_test_workflow.py`; `workflow_picker.py` tile; `workflows_mode.py` wiring; `ZPStage.py` `get/reset_comm_counters` + counters. Tests: `tests/test_v75x_stress_test_workflow.py` (16); ZP+quick-print+stress = 77 green. Use it to validate `MEBP_v75x_ZP_SERIAL_FLOW_CONTROL.md`. |
| `MEBP_v75x_ZP_SERIAL_FLOW_CONTROL.md` | v7.5.x **ROOT-CAUSE fix for the recurring "ZP disconnected" — synchronous Marlin `ok` flow control (Phase 1 of a staged plan)**. The ZP board is a Marlin controller (runs prints for hours when driven correctly); the difference was the host protocol. `ZPStage.send_data` fired G-code **blind** (`write`+`flush`, never read Marlin's `ok`), so under any dense/sustained stream it outran Marlin's small AVR serial RX (~128 B) + planner buffers → dropped/corrupt bytes → board desyncs/goes silent → surfaced as a mid-print/mid-travel disconnect. Fix: on **real hardware** `send_data(data, wait_ok=True)` now BLOCKS until Marlin acks `ok` (the handshake OctoPrint/Pronterface use). New `_txn(line,*,ok_timeout,collect)` (write→read-until-`ok`, whole round-trip under the `_serial_lock` RLock so a concurrent reader can't steal the `ok`) + `_read_until_ok` line classifier: `ok`→done; `error…`→reject; `busy`/`echo:busy`→alive, extend window; reset banner (`start`/`Marlin`/`FIRMWARE_NAME`)→**board-reset detected** (`_board_reset_detected`, position lost); silence past `DEFAULT_OK_TIMEOUT_S`(6 s)→fail (clears `_last_position_read_ok` so the poller escalates fast). `get_current_position` real-HW path rewritten to one synchronous `M114` transaction (parse position, consume `ok`) — the old "drain stale acks before M114" is gone because the buffer is now always clean. **Simulator + `wait_ok=False` keep fire-and-forget** (sim answers everything; suite relies on non-blocking writes). `ok`=admitted-to-planner-buffer (ms), NOT motion-complete, so it doesn't slow motion — `flush_moves`(M400) still waits for completion. Supersedes the symptom-patches (drain-before-M114, poller-suspend-during-PRINT_PATH, watchdog debounce) as the real fix; those stay as defense-in-depth. **Phase 2 (next, after bench-confirm): line numbers + checksum + `Resend`-on-corruption** (robust to corrupted bytes, not just overflow). Hardware still matters for a true "never" — a `ZP board RESET detected mid-session` line at the bench = brownout/auto-reset → pursue the hardware auto-reset disable (`MEBP_v75x_ZP_DTR_NO_RESET.md`) + clean USB/power. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_zp_serial_flow_control.py` (11) + updated disconnect/jog-clamp/position-override fakes; full `test_v75x*` = 717/718 (1 pre-existing unrelated CV failure). |
| `MEBP_v75x_QUICK_PRINT_PREPOSITION_ASYNC.md` | v7.5.x two bench-reported Quick Print fixes (one run, both symptoms). **(1) Live needle feed froze until ~halfway through the print** — the pre-position `safe_travel_to` ran on the **GUI thread**, blocking Qt's event loop so the microscope `CameraFeedView` couldn't repaint; normally brief but in the report it blocked the **full 15 s `wait_for_z_arrival` timeout** (board had gone silent). Fix: the pre-position move now runs on a daemon **worker thread**; `_PrintBridge` gains a `prepositioned` signal and `_on_print` validates+builds settings+stashes context in `self._pending_print` then launches the move off-thread, resuming in the queued-signal continuation `_on_prepositioned` (confirm dialog → build/start job on the GUI thread). `_update_button_state` treats an in-flight pre-position as busy so the 300 ms status tick can't re-enable Print mid-move; the continuation **re-checks `is_zp_connected`** so a drop during positioning can't dry-run a print with the needle parked down. **(2) ZP disconnected at the end and didn't move up** — a **genuine** board drop: `wait_for_z_arrival` polled `get_current_position()`, which returns the last **stale** floats when the board stops answering M114, and never checked `_last_position_read_ok` — so the lost retract G-code + 15 s of frozen `z=68.23` reads happened **before** the poller-liveness watchdog declared the disconnect. Fix (`StageController.wait_for_z_arrival`): watch `_last_position_read_ok` and **fast-fail** after a debounced streak (5 consecutive failures ≈ 0.75 s); a single transient miss under a write burst is ridden out. Proven from `logs/prints/…171814…B2.jsonl` (the 17:18 print completed normally; the failing attempt was a later pre-position). Does NOT stop the board dropping — durable cure is the hardware auto-reset disable in `MEBP_v75x_ZP_DTR_NO_RESET.md`; this makes the failure surface fast+visibly. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_quick_print_preposition_async.py` (8) + updated `test_v75x_quick_print_position_confirm.py`; Z/ZP/print regression (55) green. |
| `MEBP_v75x_XBOX_AXIS_SPEED_PERCENT.md` | v7.5.x **Xbox jog speed per axis as a PERCENTAGE of the calibrated max move speed**, cycled by the controller through the fixed ladder **0.1 / 0.3 / 1 / 3 / 10 / 30 / 100 %** (100% = the per-axis Hardware-Setup max). Reuses the already-mapped `increment_{xy,z,p}speed_up/down` controller commands (default bumpers/D-pad) — they previously stepped by **decades (×10/÷10)** with arbitrary caps; now they walk the ladder. Grouping = **XY / Z / Pump** (matches the joystick: one stick = XY together, triggers = Z/pumps), per user choice; on-screen = **read-out only** (the value changes via the controller, the page shows it; selection persists). New module `JOG_SPEED_LADDER_PCT` + `_jog_speed_ladder_step` (snaps to nearest rung, clamps at ends). In `StageController.py` the handler speed scalars become **derived**: `effective = pct/100 × speed_max_native` (`ZPJogHandler.z_speed`/`p_speed`, `XYJogHandler.xy_speed`), recomputed on any %/max change; the velocity clamp = `speed_max` so 100% reaches — never exceeds — the calibrated max (Z clamps at its own `z_speed_max`, pump caps at its 100%-flow ceiling, the per-pump `_clamp_pump_flow` still governs). New `set_*_speed_pct/max` setters on both handlers. Controller `refresh_jog_speed_limits()` sources the per-axis max exactly like the Control Panel "Speeds" (XY→`safety_limits.max_xy_speed` µm/s; Z→`per_axis_max_feedrate['Z']`/`max_z_feedrate` mm/min ÷60→mm/s; Pump→`_pump_jog_max_native()` = largest configured flow limit µL/s, else `max_pump_feedrate`÷60 mm/s) and re-applies the stored %; called after jog-handler (re)creation, at the end of `apply_device_settings`, and in `set_hardware_config`. The % is owned by the controller (`_jog_speed_pct`) so it survives (re)connect; `set_jog_speed_pct`/`get_jog_speed_state`/`_apply_jog_speed_pct`. Xbox page (`gui/pages/hardware/xbox_panel.py`): new "Jog Speed (% of calibrated max)" read-out card (XY/Z/Pump → % + resolved speed, 20 Hz from `get_jog_speed_state()`, updates even when only the stage — not the controller — is connected); restores the persisted % into the controller on `set_controller`/`set_settings`, persists controller-driven changes (`xbox.jog_speed_pct.{xy,z,p}`). **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_xbox_axis_speed_percent.py` (20); `test_v75x_xbox_input_pipeline` (19) + jog-direction/axis-map/clamp suites green. |
| `MEBP_v75x_PLATE_Z_AUTOCAL_PER_WELL_FOCUS.md` | v7.5.x **reworked the Plate Z Auto-Cal into a guided per-well refocus → confirm → needle-best-focus flow** (the old single-seed "teach first spot, auto-cal the rest" design didn't let the operator refocus on the glass per well and silently failed to find a focus peak). Now EACH calibration well: retract + travel there → operator refocuses the microscope on the glass (manual knob) and clicks **Confirm focus & lower needle** → the needle lowers (`coarse`/`fine` descent, polarity-safe via `_auto_z_move_to_h`) and the best-focus Z (sharpest needle circle = tip at the glass plane) is **auto-recorded** (peak = 3 declining focus steps; **Record now** = manual override) → operator **Accept**s (commit + advance; fit the Z plane on the last well) or **Redo**es. Descent window seeded from the previous accepted Z (tracks plate tilt) → Plate Bottom Z → `top_z − well_depth`, bounded by `_auto_z_floor_h` so a bad estimate degrades to "no peak → Redo", never a crash-down. `gui/pages/calibration.py`: rebuilt `_build_plate_z_autocal_tab` (Start/Cancel + Confirm/Record/Accept/Redo + status + live feed); `_start_auto_z_cal` drops the seed/skip logic; `_auto_z_tick` `navigate` now pauses in operator-gated `await_focus` (no needle motion until Confirm); new `_auto_z_phase_buttons`/`_auto_z_enter_await_focus`/`_zauto_confirm_focus`/`_auto_z_enter_recorded`/`_zauto_record_now`/`_zauto_accept_well`/`_zauto_redo_well`; removed `_zauto_goto_first_well`/`_zauto_record_first_spot` + seed attrs; Custom-tab "Auto Z-Cal" button (`_start_auto_z_cal_on_tab`) switches to the Plate Z Auto-Cal tab so the interactive buttons are present. Supersedes `MEBP_v75x_Z_AUTOCAL_MANUAL_SEED.md`. **Needs real-HW verification on ME3B V1.** Tests: `tests/test_v75x_plate_z_autocal_per_well_focus.py` (24) + updated `test_v75x_plate_z_autocal_tab.py` (8); removed `test_v75x_z_autocal_manual_seed.py`. |
